**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Mesh/MeshTopology.swift`**

````swift
/// What a mesh's connectivity says (implicit spec §2.2, §7): whether it is closed, manifold and consistently oriented, and its Euler
/// characteristic. The tests of every mesher check it on every mesh they make.
public struct MeshTopology: Sendable {
    /// Vertices used by at least one triangle.
    public let usedVertexCount: Int
    public let edgeCount: Int
    public let triangleCount: Int
    /// Edges with a single face.
    public let openEdgeCount: Int
    /// Edges with more than two faces.
    public let overfullEdgeCount: Int
    /// Edges whose two faces run along it the same way (a flipped face).
    public let inconsistentEdgeCount: Int
    /// Triangles with a repeated vertex.
    public let degenerateTriangleCount: Int
    /// Vertices whose faces do not form a single fan.
    public let nonManifoldVertexCount: Int

    public init(_ mesh: TriangleMesh) {
        struct EdgeKey: Hashable {
            var low: UInt32
            var high: UInt32
        }
        // Per edge: how many faces use it, and how many of them run low → high.
        var edges: [EdgeKey: (faces: Int, forward: Int)] = [:]
        var used = Set<UInt32>()
        var degenerate = 0
        var links: [UInt32: [(UInt32, UInt32)]] = [:]
        for index in 0..<mesh.triangleCount {
            let corners = [mesh.triangles[3 * index], mesh.triangles[3 * index + 1], mesh.triangles[3 * index + 2]]
            guard Set(corners).count == 3 else { degenerate += 1; continue }
            for side in 0..<3 {
                let (from, to, other) = (corners[side], corners[(side + 1) % 3], corners[(side + 2) % 3])
                used.insert(from)
                let key = EdgeKey(low: min(from, to), high: max(from, to))
                var entry = edges[key] ?? (0, 0)
                entry.faces += 1
                if from < to { entry.forward += 1 }
                edges[key] = entry
                // The link of `from`: the edge opposite it.
                links[from, default: []].append((to, other))
            }
        }
        usedVertexCount = used.count
        edgeCount = edges.count
        triangleCount = mesh.triangleCount - degenerate
        openEdgeCount = edges.values.filter { $0.faces == 1 }.count
        overfullEdgeCount = edges.values.filter { $0.faces > 2 }.count
        inconsistentEdgeCount = edges.values.filter { $0.faces == 2 && $0.forward != 1 }.count
        degenerateTriangleCount = degenerate
        nonManifoldVertexCount = links.values.filter { !Self.isSingleFan($0) }.count
    }

    /// Whether the link edges of a vertex form one closed cycle.
    private static func isSingleFan(_ link: [(UInt32, UInt32)]) -> Bool {
        var neighbours: [UInt32: [UInt32]] = [:]
        for (a, b) in link {
            neighbours[a, default: []].append(b)
            neighbours[b, default: []].append(a)
        }
        guard neighbours.values.allSatisfy({ $0.count == 2 }), let start = neighbours.keys.first else { return false }
        var seen: Set<UInt32> = [start]
        var stack = [start]
        while let next = stack.popLast() {
            for other in neighbours[next] ?? [] where seen.insert(other).inserted { stack.append(other) }
        }
        return seen.count == neighbours.count
    }

    /// V − E + F over the used vertices: 2 for a sphere, 0 for a torus.
    public var eulerCharacteristic: Int { usedVertexCount - edgeCount + triangleCount }

    /// Every edge has exactly two faces, running opposite ways, no face is degenerate and every vertex has one fan.
    public var isClosedManifold: Bool {
        openEdgeCount == 0 && overfullEdgeCount == 0 && inconsistentEdgeCount == 0 && degenerateTriangleCount == 0
            && nonManifoldVertexCount == 0 && triangleCount > 0
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Mesh/TriangleMesh.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import Foundation

/// A triangle mesh (implicit spec §2.2): `positions` in millimetres (stored as `Float`), `triangles` three indices per face,
/// counter-clockwise seen from outside, and a unit `normals` entry per vertex (empty when unknown). Immutable and `Sendable`
/// (arrays are copy-on-write, so a copy is cheap), never saved. A mesh from the Mesh node is watertight and manifold: every edge has
/// exactly two faces, consistently oriented.
public struct TriangleMesh: MeshPayload, Sendable {
    /// Identifies this mesh, so a refined pass can replace the one it came from. A new mesh gets a new ID.
    public let id: UUID
    public var positions: [SIMD3<Float>]
    public var normals: [SIMD3<Float>]
    public var triangles: [UInt32]
    /// The voxel size it was made at, in millimetres; 0 when unknown.
    public var resolution: Double
    public var quality: MeshQuality
    public var highlights: [MeshHighlight]
    /// What a preview refinement meshes, when this mesh is a preview of an implicit.
    public var source: MeshSource?

    public init(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], normals: [SIMD3<Float>] = [], triangles: [UInt32], resolution: Double = 0,
                quality: MeshQuality = .preview, highlights: [MeshHighlight] = [], source: MeshSource? = nil, id: UUID = UUID()) {
        self.id = id
        self.positions = positions
        self.normals = normals
        self.triangles = triangles
        self.resolution = resolution
        self.quality = quality
        self.highlights = highlights
        self.source = source
    }

    public var vertexCount: Int { positions.count }
    public var triangleCount: Int { triangles.count / 3 }

    /// 16 per vertex and 12 per triangle (implicit spec §2.2).
    public var estimatedBytes: Int { 16 * positions.count + 12 * triangleCount }

    /// The three corners of triangle `index`.
    public func corners(of index: Int) -> (Vector3, Vector3, Vector3) {
        (point(Int(triangles[3 * index])), point(Int(triangles[3 * index + 1])), point(Int(triangles[3 * index + 2])))
    }

    public func point(_ vertex: Int) -> Vector3 {
        let p = positions[vertex]
        return Vector3(Double(p.x), Double(p.y), Double(p.z))
    }

    /// The box around the vertices, or `nil` for an empty mesh.
    public var bounds: BoundingBox? { BoundingBox(points: positions.map { Vector3(Double($0.x), Double($0.y), Double($0.z)) }) }

    /// The enclosed volume in cubic millimetres: positive for a closed mesh wound counter-clockwise seen from outside.
    public var signedVolume: Double {
        var sum = 0.0
        for index in 0..<triangleCount {
            let (a, b, c) = corners(of: index)
            sum += a.dot(b.cross(c))
        }
        return sum / 6
    }

    /// The area of all faces in square millimetres.
    public var surfaceArea: Double {
        var sum = 0.0
        for index in 0..<triangleCount {
            let (a, b, c) = corners(of: index)
            sum += (b - a).cross(c - a).length / 2
        }
        return sum
    }

    /// This mesh with other highlights (checks return their input flagged).
    public func highlighting(_ highlights: [MeshHighlight]) -> TriangleMesh {
        var copy = self
        copy.highlights = highlights
        return copy
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/DistanceBlocks.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry

/// A baked signed distance on a regular grid, stored sparsely (implicit spec §4.4): blocks of 8 × 8 × 8 `Float` samples, kept only
/// where the surface is within `band` of them, and for every other block one conservative value (a lower bound of the distance
/// there, with the sign of the side it is on). Within a block the value is trilinear between samples, so it is Lipschitz 1 to within
/// the interpolation error `spacing` is chosen to keep under the tolerance. Immutable; equal by identity.
public final class DistanceBlocks: Hashable, Sendable {
    /// Samples per block along each axis.
    public static let blockSize = 8

    /// World position of sample (0, 0, 0).
    public let origin: Vector3
    /// Distance between neighbouring samples, in millimetres.
    public let spacing: Double
    /// Blocks along x, y and z.
    public let blockCounts: [Int]
    /// For each block (x fastest, then y, then z): its place in `pool` (a block number), or -1 when it is not stored.
    public let table: [Int32]
    /// The stored blocks' samples, 512 per block (x fastest).
    public let pool: [Float]
    /// For each block: the signed lower bound used where it is not stored.
    public let coarse: [Float]
    /// Distances up to this size from the surface are stored exactly; beyond it the value is a bound.
    public let band: Double

    public init(origin: Vector3, spacing: Double, blockCounts: [Int], table: [Int32], pool: [Float], coarse: [Float], band: Double) {
        precondition(blockCounts.count == 3 && table.count == blockCounts.reduce(1, *) && coarse.count == table.count,
                     "DistanceBlocks needs one table and one coarse entry per block")
        self.origin = origin
        self.spacing = spacing
        self.blockCounts = blockCounts
        self.table = table
        self.pool = pool
        self.coarse = coarse
        self.band = band
    }

    public static func == (lhs: DistanceBlocks, rhs: DistanceBlocks) -> Bool { lhs === rhs }
    public func hash(into hasher: inout Hasher) { hasher.combine(ObjectIdentifier(self)) }

    /// The size of one block in millimetres.
    public var blockLength: Double { Double(Self.blockSize) * spacing }

    /// The box the samples span: from the first sample to the last.
    public var bounds: BoundingBox {
        let counts = sampleCounts
        let size = Vector3(Double(counts[0] - 1), Double(counts[1] - 1), Double(counts[2] - 1)) * spacing
        return BoundingBox(min: origin, max: origin + size)
    }

    /// How many blocks are stored.
    public var storedBlockCount: Int { pool.count / (Self.blockSize * Self.blockSize * Self.blockSize) }

    /// The memory the tables take (implicit spec §2.1: baked blocks count towards `estimatedBytes`).
    public var estimatedBytes: Int { pool.count * 4 + table.count * 8 + 128 }

    private var sampleCounts: [Int] { blockCounts.map { $0 * Self.blockSize } }

    private func blockNumber(_ bx: Int, _ by: Int, _ bz: Int) -> Int { (bz * blockCounts[1] + by) * blockCounts[0] + bx }

    /// The sample at integer grid position (i, j, k), held to the grid; where its block is not stored, the block's bound.
    func sample(_ i: Int, _ j: Int, _ k: Int) -> Double {
        let counts = sampleCounts
        let (ci, cj, ck) = (min(max(i, 0), counts[0] - 1), min(max(j, 0), counts[1] - 1), min(max(k, 0), counts[2] - 1))
        let size = Self.blockSize
        let block = blockNumber(ci / size, cj / size, ck / size)
        let slot = Int(table[block])
        guard slot >= 0 else { return Double(coarse[block]) }
        let local = ((ck % size) * size + (cj % size)) * size + (ci % size)
        return Double(pool[slot * size * size * size + local])
    }

    /// The baked signed distance at `point`. Inside the grid: trilinear between samples. Outside it: the value at the nearest point of
    /// the grid plus the distance to it (the grid is padded past the surface, so outside it is outside the solid).
    public func value(at point: Vector3) -> Double {
        let box = bounds
        let held = Vector3(min(max(point.x, box.min.x), box.max.x), min(max(point.y, box.min.y), box.max.y),
                           min(max(point.z, box.min.z), box.max.z))
        let outside = (point - held).length
        let grid = (held - origin) * (1 / spacing)
        let counts = sampleCounts
        // Sample centres sit on grid points; clamp the cell so the last sample's neighbour is itself.
        let (fx, fy, fz) = (min(max(grid.x, 0), Double(counts[0] - 1)), min(max(grid.y, 0), Double(counts[1] - 1)),
                            min(max(grid.z, 0), Double(counts[2] - 1)))
        let (ix, iy, iz) = (Int(fx.rounded(.down)), Int(fy.rounded(.down)), Int(fz.rounded(.down)))
        let (tx, ty, tz) = (fx - Double(ix), fy - Double(iy), fz - Double(iz))
        func mix(_ a: Double, _ b: Double, _ t: Double) -> Double { a + (b - a) * t }
        let x00 = mix(sample(ix, iy, iz), sample(ix + 1, iy, iz), tx)
        let x10 = mix(sample(ix, iy + 1, iz), sample(ix + 1, iy + 1, iz), tx)
        let x01 = mix(sample(ix, iy, iz + 1), sample(ix + 1, iy, iz + 1), tx)
        let x11 = mix(sample(ix, iy + 1, iz + 1), sample(ix + 1, iy + 1, iz + 1), tx)
        return mix(mix(x00, x10, ty), mix(x01, x11, ty), tz) + outside
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/Grading.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// A setting that may vary over space: a blend radius, a shell thickness, an offset (implicit spec §3.3). The field is sampled
/// per point by the implicit's own evaluator, never through `FieldSampler`. Distances to a solid, face set or edge set need the
/// kernel, so each is baked beforehand (`GradingBaker`) into `baked`, which the evaluator looks up by target.
public struct Grading: Hashable, Sendable {
    /// A distance to a kernel target, baked on a grid.
    public struct Baked: Hashable, Sendable {
        public var target: DistanceTarget.Identity
        public var signed: Bool
        public var blocks: DistanceBlocks

        public init(target: DistanceTarget.Identity, signed: Bool, blocks: DistanceBlocks) {
            self.target = target
            self.signed = signed
            self.blocks = blocks
        }
    }

    public var field: Field
    public var baked: [Baked]
    /// Every value the field takes over the region it was made for (rigorous: `GradingBaker` adds the field's slope times the
    /// half-diagonal of its sampling cells to the extremes it found).
    public var range: ClosedRange<Double>
    /// A bound on how fast the field changes, in value per millimetre (`Field.slopeBound`).
    public var slope: Double

    public init(field: Field, baked: [Baked] = [], range: ClosedRange<Double>, slope: Double) {
        self.field = field
        self.baked = baked
        self.range = range
        self.slope = slope
    }

    /// The same value everywhere.
    public static func constant(_ value: Double) -> Grading {
        Grading(field: .constant(value), range: value...value, slope: 0)
    }

    /// The value when it is the same everywhere.
    public var constantValue: Double? {
        if case .constant(let value) = field { value } else { nil }
    }

    /// The largest absolute value.
    public var magnitude: Double { max(abs(range.lowerBound), abs(range.upperBound)) }

    /// The baked blocks for a distance leaf, if it was baked.
    public func blocks(for target: DistanceTarget.Identity, signed: Bool) -> DistanceBlocks? {
        baked.first { $0.target == target && $0.signed == signed }?.blocks
    }

    public var estimatedBytes: Int { field.estimatedBytes + baked.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.blocks.estimatedBytes } }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Bounds.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry

extension ImplicitSolid {
    /// A box that contains the surface (implicit spec §2.1). A smooth union may bulge a quarter of its blend radius past its
    /// children, an offset or a shell grows by its largest distance, and an intersection can be empty (then a zero-size box at the
    /// first child's centre).
    public var bounds: BoundingBox {
        switch self {
        case .sphere(let radius):
            return Self.box(Vector3(radius, radius, radius))
        case .box(let half, _):
            return Self.box(half)
        case .cylinder(let radius, let halfHeight):
            return Self.box(Vector3(radius, radius, halfHeight))
        case .moved(let child, let move):
            return move.bounds(of: child.bounds)
        case .union(let children, let blend):
            let all = children.map(\.bounds)
            let joined = all.dropFirst().reduce(all.first ?? Self.box(.zero)) { $0.union($1) }
            return Self.grown(joined, by: max(0, blend.range.upperBound) / 4)
        case .intersect(let children, _):
            let all = children.map(\.bounds)
            guard let first = all.first else { return Self.box(.zero) }
            let overlap = all.dropFirst().reduce(Optional(first)) { box, next in box.flatMap { next.intersection($0) } }
            return overlap ?? BoundingBox(min: first.center, max: first.center)
        case .subtract(let children, _):
            return children.first?.bounds ?? Self.box(.zero)
        case .offset(let child, let distance):
            return Self.grown(child.bounds, by: max(0, distance.range.upperBound))
        case .shell(let child, let thickness, let side):
            let largest = max(0, thickness.range.upperBound)
            switch side {
            case .inward: return child.bounds
            case .outward: return Self.grown(child.bounds, by: largest)
            case .centred: return Self.grown(child.bounds, by: largest / 2)
            }
        case .baked(let blocks, _):
            return blocks.bounds
        case .isosurface(_, _, _, let region):
            return region
        }
    }

    private static func box(_ half: Vector3) -> BoundingBox { BoundingBox(min: -half, max: half) }

    static func grown(_ box: BoundingBox, by amount: Double) -> BoundingBox {
        let pad = Vector3(amount, amount, amount)
        return BoundingBox(min: box.min - pad, max: box.max + pad)
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Description.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation

extension ImplicitSolid: CustomStringConvertible {
    /// A one-line recipe like `Field.description` (implicit spec §2.1), such as
    /// `intersect(shell(implicit(Bracket), inward 2 mm), sphere(radius 40 mm))`. The inspector shows it; a socket's tooltip shows
    /// `description(limit: 80)`. Numbers are written in `Locale.messages`, millimetres with a "mm".
    public var description: String {
        switch self {
        case .sphere(let radius):
            return "sphere(radius \(Self.mm(radius)))"
        case .box(let half, let cornerRadius):
            let size = [half.x, half.y, half.z].map { ($0 * 2).millimetreText(locale: .messages) }.joined(separator: " × ")
            return "box(\(size) mm" + (cornerRadius > 0 ? ", corner radius \(Self.mm(cornerRadius)))" : ")")
        case .cylinder(let radius, let halfHeight):
            return "cylinder(radius \(Self.mm(radius)), height \(Self.mm(halfHeight * 2)))"
        case .moved(let child, _):
            return "place(\(child.description))"
        case .union(let children, let blend):
            return Self.combination("union", children, blend)
        case .subtract(let children, let blend):
            return Self.combination("subtract", children, blend)
        case .intersect(let children, let blend):
            return Self.combination("intersect", children, blend)
        case .offset(let child, let distance):
            return "offset(\(child.description), \(Self.text(distance)))"
        case .shell(let child, let thickness, let side):
            return "shell(\(child.description), \(side.name.lowercased()) \(Self.text(thickness)))"
        case .baked(_, let label):
            return "implicit(\(label))"
        case .isosurface(let grading, let level, let maxSlope, _):
            return "isosurface(\(grading.field.description), level \(level.millimetreText(locale: .messages)), "
                + "max slope \(maxSlope.millimetreText(locale: .messages)))"
        }
    }

    /// The recipe cut to `limit` characters, ending in "…" when it was cut.
    public func description(limit: Int) -> String {
        let text = description
        guard text.count > limit else { return text }
        return String(text.prefix(max(limit - 1, 0))) + "…"
    }

    private static func combination(_ name: String, _ children: [ImplicitSolid], _ blend: Grading) -> String {
        let inner = children.map(\.description).joined(separator: ", ")
        if let value = blend.constantValue, value == 0 { return "\(name)(\(inner))" }
        return "\(name)(\(inner), blend \(text(blend)))"
    }

    /// A setting: a constant in millimetres, else the field's recipe.
    private static func text(_ grading: Grading) -> String {
        grading.constantValue.map(mm) ?? grading.field.description
    }

    /// A size in millimetres, with a true minus sign.
    private static func mm(_ value: Double) -> String {
        "\(value.millimetreText(locale: .messages).replacing("-", with: "−")) mm"
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Lipschitz.swift`**

````swift
extension ImplicitSolid {
    /// A constant L ≥ 1 with |f(a) − f(b)| ≤ L · |a − b| (implicit spec §2.1, §3.3). Primitives, moves and baked distances are
    /// exact. A smooth blend takes the largest of its children's plus a quarter of the blend radius's slope (the blend term
    /// k·h²/4 changes by at most a quarter per unit of k). An offset or a shell takes its child's times one plus the slope of its
    /// distance. An isosurface divides the field's slope by `maxSlope`.
    public var lipschitz: Double {
        switch self {
        case .sphere, .box, .cylinder, .baked:
            return 1
        case .moved(let child, _):
            return child.lipschitz
        case .union(let children, let blend), .intersect(let children, let blend), .subtract(let children, let blend):
            return max(1, children.map(\.lipschitz).max() ?? 1) + blend.slope / 4
        case .offset(let child, let distance):
            return child.lipschitz * (1 + distance.slope)
        case .shell(let child, let thickness, _):
            return child.lipschitz * (1 + thickness.slope)
        case .isosurface(let grading, _, let maxSlope, _):
            return max(1, grading.slope / maxSlope)
        }
    }

    /// How far from the surface the value is the true distance (infinite for an analytic solid). A baked distance is exact within
    /// its band, and an offset or a shell reads the child that far plus its own distance, so they shorten it. Shell and Offset warn
    /// when their distance reaches past it.
    public var exactReach: Double {
        switch self {
        case .sphere, .box, .cylinder, .isosurface:
            return .infinity
        case .moved(let child, _):
            return child.exactReach
        case .union(let children, _), .intersect(let children, _), .subtract(let children, _):
            return children.map(\.exactReach).min() ?? .infinity
        case .offset(let child, let distance):
            return max(0, child.exactReach - distance.magnitude)
        case .shell(let child, let thickness, _):
            return max(0, child.exactReach - thickness.magnitude)
        case .baked(let blocks, _):
            return blocks.band
        }
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Structure.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry

extension ImplicitSolid {
    /// The solids this one is made from, in order. No `default`: a new case must say what its operands are.
    public var children: [ImplicitSolid] {
        switch self {
        case .sphere, .box, .cylinder, .baked, .isosurface:
            []
        case .moved(let child, _), .offset(let child, _), .shell(let child, _, _):
            [child]
        case .subtract(let children, _), .union(let children, _), .intersect(let children, _):
            children
        }
    }

    /// The settings that vary over space, in order.
    var gradings: [Grading] {
        switch self {
        case .sphere, .box, .cylinder, .moved, .baked:
            []
        case .subtract(_, let blend), .union(_, let blend), .intersect(_, let blend):
            [blend]
        case .offset(_, let distance):
            [distance]
        case .shell(_, let thickness, _):
            [thickness]
        case .isosurface(let grading, _, _, _):
            [grading]
        }
    }

    /// How many nodes the tree has (itself and everything under it).
    public var nodeCount: Int { 1 + children.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.nodeCount } }

    /// A rough memory cost (implicit spec §2.1): 64 plus 32 per node, plus the bytes of every baked block table and every field
    /// the settings hold.
    public var estimatedBytes: Int { 64 + 32 * nodeCount + heldBytes }

    private var heldBytes: Int {
        var own = gradings.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.estimatedBytes }
        if case .baked(let blocks, _) = self { own += blocks.estimatedBytes }
        return own + children.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.heldBytes }
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph

/// An implicit solid (implicit spec §2.1): an immutable tree whose value at a point is a signed distance in millimetres, negative
/// inside and zero on the surface (the sign of fields spec D5). Every node has `bounds`, a box that contains its surface, and
/// `lipschitz`, a constant L ≥ 1 with |f(a) − f(b)| ≤ L · |a − b|. The sparse evaluator divides by L before pruning
/// (implicit spec §4.2), so an overestimate costs speed, never correctness. `ImplicitSolid+Evaluation` evaluates it, and
/// `ImplicitCompiler` turns it into Metal.
///
/// Adding a case means adding it to `children`, `bounds`, `lipschitz`, `recipe`, the CPU evaluator and the compiler, none of
/// which has a `default`, so the compiler lists the places.
public indirect enum ImplicitSolid: Hashable, Sendable {
    // MARK: Primitives (exact distances, lipschitz 1)

    /// A ball about the origin.
    case sphere(radius: Double)
    /// A box about the origin with rounded edges: `halfSize` is half its extent on each axis, `cornerRadius` ≤ the smallest half size.
    case box(halfSize: Vector3, cornerRadius: Double)
    /// A cylinder about the origin with its axis along Z.
    case cylinder(radius: Double, halfHeight: Double)

    // MARK: Moves

    /// The solid seen through a rigid move (Place): its value at p is the child's at `move.toLocal(p)`. Exact.
    case moved(ImplicitSolid, RigidMove)

    // MARK: Combining

    /// The first child with every other child cut away; with one child, that child. `blend` rounds the joins (the polynomial
    /// smooth minimum); constant 0 is sharp.
    case subtract([ImplicitSolid], blend: Grading)
    case union([ImplicitSolid], blend: Grading)
    case intersect([ImplicitSolid], blend: Grading)
    /// The surface moved outwards by `distance` (inwards when negative).
    case offset(ImplicitSolid, distance: Grading)
    /// A wall of `thickness` on `side` of the surface.
    case shell(ImplicitSolid, thickness: Grading, side: ShellSide)

    // MARK: Conversions

    /// A solid's signed distance baked on a grid (To Implicit, implicit spec §4.4). `label` names the solid in `description`.
    case baked(DistanceBlocks, label: String)
    /// Where `grading.field` is below `level`: the value is (field − level) ÷ `maxSlope`, so lipschitz is 1 when the slope bound is
    /// honest (Isosurface). `region` bounds the surface.
    case isosurface(Grading, level: Double, maxSlope: Double, region: BoundingBox)
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/RigidMove.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry

/// A rigid move as a frame: where the local origin goes and the world directions of the local axes (orthonormal, right-handed).
/// Place on implicits (implicit spec §3.3) turns each placement plane into one, exactly as `Plane.placement` does for solids: the
/// shape, built at the origin facing +Z, ends up on the plane facing along its normal.
public struct RigidMove: Hashable, Sendable {
    public var origin: Vector3
    public var xAxis: Vector3
    public var yAxis: Vector3
    public var zAxis: Vector3

    public init(origin: Vector3, xAxis: Vector3, yAxis: Vector3, zAxis: Vector3) {
        self.origin = origin
        self.xAxis = xAxis
        self.yAxis = yAxis
        self.zAxis = zAxis
    }

    public static let identity = RigidMove(origin: .zero, xAxis: .unitX, yAxis: .unitY, zAxis: .unitZ)

    /// The move onto `plane`: +X to its x axis (made perpendicular to the normal first), +Z to its normal. `nil` for a plane with no
    /// usable direction (a zero or non-finite normal, an x axis along the normal, a non-finite origin).
    public init?(plane: Plane) {
        guard plane.origin.isFinite, plane.normal.isFinite, plane.xAxis.isFinite, let z = plane.normal.normalized,
              let x = (plane.xAxis - z * plane.xAxis.dot(z)).normalized else { return nil }
        self.init(origin: plane.origin, xAxis: x, yAxis: z.cross(x), zAxis: z)
    }

    /// Where the local point goes.
    public func toWorld(_ local: Vector3) -> Vector3 {
        origin + xAxis * local.x + yAxis * local.y + zAxis * local.z
    }

    /// The local point that goes to `world`.
    public func toLocal(_ world: Vector3) -> Vector3 {
        let offset = world - origin
        return Vector3(offset.dot(xAxis), offset.dot(yAxis), offset.dot(zAxis))
    }

    /// The box that contains the move of every corner of `box`.
    public func bounds(of box: BoundingBox) -> BoundingBox {
        let corners = (0..<8).map { bit in
            toWorld(Vector3(bit & 1 == 0 ? box.min.x : box.max.x, bit & 2 == 0 ? box.min.y : box.max.y,
                            bit & 4 == 0 ? box.min.z : box.max.z))
        }
        return BoundingBox(points: corners) ?? box
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ShellSide.swift`**

````swift
/// Which side of the surface a Shell's wall lies on (implicit spec §3.3).
public enum ShellSide: Int, Hashable, Sendable, CaseIterable {
    /// The wall is inside the surface (the solid keeps its outline).
    case inward = 0
    /// The wall is outside the surface.
    case outward = 1
    /// The wall is centred on the surface.
    case centred = 2

    /// The names the Shell node's segmented control shows, in raw-value order.
    public static let options = ["Inward", "Outward", "Centred"]

    /// The wall's centre line as a signed distance from the surface: the wall is the points within half the thickness of it.
    /// Negative is inside.
    public func centreOffset(thickness: Double) -> Double {
        switch self {
        case .inward: -thickness / 2
        case .outward: thickness / 2
        case .centred: 0
        }
    }

    public var name: String { Self.options[rawValue] }
}
````

### Task 3: The CPU reference evaluator

Evaluate every node of the tree in `Double` (`CPUEvaluation`), sample the field settings per point, make `ImplicitSolid` an `ImplicitPayload`, and prove bounds and lipschitz constants by property tests. `FieldNoise`, `SeededRandom`, `ProfileDistance` and the field maths move from CreatorNodes into CreatorGeometry (public), because the evaluator needs them and CreatorImplicit sits below CreatorNodes.

**Files:**

- Create: `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Fields/FieldMath.swift`
- Move: `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Fields/FieldNoise.swift` (from `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/FieldNoise.swift`)
- Move: `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Fields/ProfileDistance.swift` (from `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/ProfileDistance.swift`)
- Move: `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Random/SeededRandom.swift` (from `Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Variation/SeededRandom.swift`)
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Implicit/ImplicitPayload.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Evaluation/CPUEvaluation.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Evaluation/ImplicitSolid+Evaluation.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/DistanceBlocks.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Bounds.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Solid/ImplicitSolid+Lipschitz.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/FieldSampler+Maths.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/FieldSampler.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/NoiseFieldNode.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Variation/RandomReduceNode.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Variation/RandomReduction.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Variation/RandomValuesNode.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/CPUEvaluationTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/DistanceBlocksTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ImplicitSolidPropertyTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/Support/ImplicitFixtures.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Variation/SeededRandomTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `ImplicitSolid`, `Grading`, `DistanceBlocks.value(at:)`, `RigidMove.toLocal` (Task 2); `Field` (Task 1).
- Produces (the public declarations this task adds; later tasks use these exact names):

````swift
public enum FieldMath
public static func remap(_ value: Double, fromLow: Double, fromHigh: Double, toLow: Double, toHigh: Double,
public static func minimum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double) -> Double
public static func maximum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double) -> Double
public static func clamp(_ value: Double, low: Double, high: Double) -> Double
public static func smoothMinimum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double, width: Double) -> Double
public struct FieldNoise: Sendable
public let scale: Double
public let octaves: Int
public init(seed: Int, scale: Double, octaves: Int)
public func value(at point: Vector3) -> Double
public struct ProfileDistance: Sendable
public init(_ profile: Profile2D)
public func distance(from point: Vector3) -> Double
public struct SeededRandom: Sendable
public static let step: UInt64 = 0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15
public enum Purpose: UInt64, Sendable
public init(seed: Int, stream: Int = 0, purpose: Purpose = .plain)
public static func mix(_ value: UInt64) -> UInt64
public func value(at index: Int) -> UInt64
public mutating func next() -> UInt64
public func unit(at index: Int) -> Double
public func below(_ bound: Int, at index: Int) -> Int
public var summary: String
public var payloadBounds: BoundingBox
public func signedDistance(at point: Vector3) -> Double
public func signedDistances(at points: [Vector3]) -> [Double]
public var reference: ImplicitReference
public var solid: ImplicitSolid?
````


**Notes for the implementer:**

- The moved files keep their tests: `FieldNoiseTests`, `SeededRandomTests` and `ProfileDistanceTests` stay in CreatorNodesTests and only gain `import CreatorGeometry`.
- `FieldSampler`'s remap, clamp, min, max and smooth-minimum now forward to `FieldMath`, so the sampler and the evaluator give the same number.
- An isosurface is clipped to its region: its value is `max((field - level) / maxSlope, distance to the region box)`, which is what makes `bounds` honest for a field that is negative everywhere.

**Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/CPUEvaluationTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// The reference distances at hand-worked points (implicit spec §7).
struct CPUEvaluationTests {
    func distance(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, _ x: Double, _ y: Double, _ z: Double) -> Double {
        solid.signedDistance(at: Vector3(x, y, z))
    }

    /// `solid` moved `x` millimetres along X.
    func moved(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, x: Double) -> ImplicitSolid {
        .moved(solid, RigidMove(origin: Vector3(x, 0, 0), xAxis: .unitX, yAxis: .unitY, zAxis: .unitZ))
    }

    func near(_ a: Double, _ b: Double, _ tolerance: Double = 1e-9) -> Bool { abs(a - b) <= tolerance }

    @Test func aSphereIsTheDistanceFromItsCentreLessItsRadius() {
        let sphere = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 10)
        #expect(distance(sphere, 0, 0, 0) == -10)
        #expect(distance(sphere, 10, 0, 0) == 0)
        #expect(near(distance(sphere, 3, 4, 12), 13 - 10))
    }

    @Test func aBoxIsExactOutsideAlongFacesEdgesAndCorners() {
        let box = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(5, 5, 5), cornerRadius: 0)
        #expect(distance(box, 0, 0, 0) == -5)
        #expect(near(distance(box, 8, 0, 0), 3))
        #expect(near(distance(box, 8, 8, 0), 18.0.squareRoot()))
        #expect(near(distance(box, 8, 9, 11), (9 + 16 + 36.0).squareRoot()))
        #expect(near(distance(box, 4, 1, 2), -1), "inside: the nearest face")
    }

    @Test func aRoundedBoxCutsItsCorners() {
        let box = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(5, 5, 5), cornerRadius: 1)
        #expect(near(distance(box, 5, 5, 5), 3.0.squareRoot() - 1))
        #expect(near(distance(box, 8, 0, 0), 3), "faces are unchanged")
        // Past the rounded edge: the edge's centre line is at (4, 4), the point is √5 from it, less the radius 1.
        #expect(near(distance(box, 6, 5, 0), 5.0.squareRoot() - 1))
    }

    @Test func aCylinderHasItsAxisAlongZ() {
        let cylinder = ImplicitSolid.cylinder(radius: 3, halfHeight: 5)
        #expect(distance(cylinder, 0, 0, 0) == -3)
        #expect(near(distance(cylinder, 5, 0, 0), 2))
        #expect(near(distance(cylinder, 0, 0, 8), 3))
        #expect(near(distance(cylinder, 5, 0, 8), 13.0.squareRoot()))
        #expect(near(distance(cylinder, 0, 2, 4.5), -0.5))
    }

    @Test func aMoveSeesTheChildThroughTheInverse() throws {
        let move = try #require(RigidMove(plane: Plane(origin: Vector3(10, 0, 0), normal: .unitZ, xAxis: .unitX)))
        let moved = ImplicitSolid.moved(.sphere(radius: 4), move)
        #expect(distance(moved, 10, 0, 0) == -4)
        #expect(near(distance(moved, 0, 0, 0), 6))
        let alongX = try #require(RigidMove(plane: Plane(origin: .zero, normal: .unitX, xAxis: .unitY)))
        let turned = ImplicitSolid.moved(.cylinder(radius: 1, halfHeight: 5), alongX)
        // The axis now runs along X.
        #expect(near(distance(turned, 4, 0, 0), -1) && near(distance(turned, 0, 3, 0), 2))
    }

    @Test func aSharpUnionIsTheSmallerValue() {
        let union = ImplicitSolid.union([.sphere(radius: 5), moved(.sphere(radius: 5), x: 8)], blend: .constant(0))
        #expect(near(distance(union, 4, 0, 0), -1))
        #expect(near(distance(union, 0, 0, 0), -5))
    }

    @Test func aSmoothUnionDipsByAQuarterOfTheBlendWhereTheChildrenMeet() {
        let two = [ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 5), moved(.sphere(radius: 5), x: 10)]
        // At the midpoint both children read 0, so a width of 2 gives 0 − 2 / 4.
        #expect(near(distance(.union(two, blend: .constant(2)), 5, 0, 0), -0.5))
        #expect(near(distance(.union(two, blend: .constant(0)), 5, 0, 0), 0))
        // Far from the join the blend does nothing.
        #expect(near(distance(.union(two, blend: .constant(2)), 0, 0, 0), -5))
    }

    @Test func subtractCutsEveryLaterChildFromTheFirst() {
        let hole = moved(.sphere(radius: 5), x: 10)
        let cut = ImplicitSolid.subtract([.sphere(radius: 10), hole], blend: .constant(0))
        #expect(near(distance(cut, 10, 0, 0), 5), "the centre of the cutter is outside the result")
        #expect(near(distance(cut, -5, 0, 0), -5))
        #expect(near(distance(.subtract([.sphere(radius: 10)], blend: .constant(0)), 0, 0, 0), -10), "one child is that child")
    }

    @Test func intersectKeepsWhatEveryChildHas() {
        let box = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(5, 5, 5), cornerRadius: 0)
        let both = ImplicitSolid.intersect([.sphere(radius: 6), box], blend: .constant(0))
        #expect(near(distance(both, 5.5, 0, 0), 0.5), "outside the box, inside the sphere")
        #expect(near(distance(both, 0, 0, 0), -5))
    }

    @Test func anOffsetMovesTheSurface() {
        #expect(near(distance(.offset(.sphere(radius: 10), distance: .constant(2)), 12, 0, 0), 0))
        #expect(near(distance(.offset(.sphere(radius: 10), distance: .constant(-2)), 8, 0, 0), 0))
    }

    @Test func aShellIsAWallOnTheChosenSide() {
        let sphere = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 10)
        let inward = ImplicitSolid.shell(sphere, thickness: .constant(2), side: .inward)
        #expect(near(distance(inward, 10, 0, 0), 0) && near(distance(inward, 8, 0, 0), 0))
        #expect(near(distance(inward, 9, 0, 0), -1) && near(distance(inward, 0, 0, 0), 8))
        let outward = ImplicitSolid.shell(sphere, thickness: .constant(2), side: .outward)
        #expect(near(distance(outward, 12, 0, 0), 0) && near(distance(outward, 11, 0, 0), -1) && near(distance(outward, 9, 0, 0), 1))
        let centred = ImplicitSolid.shell(sphere, thickness: .constant(2), side: .centred)
        #expect(near(distance(centred, 10, 0, 0), -1) && near(distance(centred, 11, 0, 0), 0) && near(distance(centred, 9, 0, 0), 0))
    }

    @Test func aGradedSettingIsReadAtEachPoint() {
        // Thickness 1 + z / 20: 1.5 at z = 10, 0.5 at z = −10.
        let box = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(30, 30, 30), cornerRadius: 0)
        let shell = ImplicitSolid.shell(box, thickness: ImplicitFixtures.graded, side: .inward)
        // At z = 29.25 the thickness is 1 + 29.25 / 20 = 2.4625 and the box surface is 0.75 away, inside the wall's middle.
        let thickness = 1 + 29.25 / 20
        #expect(near(distance(shell, 0, 0, 29.25), abs(-0.75 + thickness / 2) - thickness / 2))
    }

    @Test func aBakedSolidReadsItsBlocks() {
        let value = distance(ImplicitFixtures.baked, 3, 4, 0)
        #expect(near(value, 5 - 9, 1e-5))
    }

    @Test func anIsosurfaceIsTheFieldBelowItsLevelClippedToItsRegion() {
        let solid = ImplicitFixtures.isosurface
        #expect(near(distance(solid, 10, 0, 0), 0, 1e-9) && near(distance(solid, 0, 0, 0), -10, 1e-9))
        #expect(near(distance(solid, 14, 0, 0), 4, 1e-9))
        // Past the region the box distance wins: the field alone would say 20 − 10 = 10, the box 5.
        #expect(near(distance(solid, 20, 0, 0), 10, 1e-9))
        #expect(near(distance(solid, 0, 0, 0), -10, 1e-9))
    }

    @Test func aFieldLeafOfAnImplicitIsItsOwnDistance() {
        let inner = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 3)
        let grading = Grading(field: .implicit(inner.reference), range: -3...20, slope: 1)
        let shell = ImplicitSolid.offset(.sphere(radius: 10), distance: grading)
        // At (10, 0, 0) the inner sphere reads 7, so the offset is sphere − 7.
        #expect(near(distance(shell, 10, 0, 0), 0 - 7))
    }

    @Test func batchesAgreeWithSinglePoints() {
        let solid = ImplicitFixtures.all[6].solid
        let points = (0..<20).map { Vector3(Double($0) - 10, Double($0 % 5), Double($0 % 3)) }
        #expect(solid.signedDistances(at: points) == points.map { solid.signedDistance(at: $0) })
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ImplicitSolidPropertyTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// Properties every node must have (implicit spec §7): bounds contain the surface, and the lipschitz constant holds on random
/// point pairs.
struct ImplicitSolidPropertyTests {
    /// Points from a seeded generator, so a failure is the same every time.
    static func points(in box: BoundingBox, count: Int, stream: Int) -> [Vector3] {
        let random = SeededRandom(seed: 7, stream: stream)
        return (0..<count).map { index in
            Vector3(box.min.x + box.size.x * random.unit(at: 3 * index), box.min.y + box.size.y * random.unit(at: 3 * index + 1),
                    box.min.z + box.size.z * random.unit(at: 3 * index + 2))
        }
    }

    @Test(arguments: ImplicitFixtures.all.map(\.name))
    func nothingIsInsideOutsideTheBounds(_ name: String) throws {
        let solid = try #require(ImplicitFixtures.all.first { $0.name == name }?.solid)
        let box = solid.bounds
        let region = ImplicitSolid.grown(box, by: 6)
        let outside = Self.points(in: region, count: 4000, stream: 1).filter { point in
            point.x < box.min.x || point.x > box.max.x || point.y < box.min.y || point.y > box.max.y
                || point.z < box.min.z || point.z > box.max.z
        }
        #expect(outside.count > 500)
        let values = solid.signedDistances(at: outside)
        #expect(values.allSatisfy { $0 > -1e-9 }, "\(name): the smallest value outside the bounds is \(values.min() ?? 0)")
    }

    @Test(arguments: ImplicitFixtures.all.map(\.name))
    func theLipschitzConstantHoldsOnRandomPairs(_ name: String) throws {
        let solid = try #require(ImplicitFixtures.all.first { $0.name == name }?.solid)
        let region = ImplicitSolid.grown(solid.bounds, by: 3)
        let first = Self.points(in: region, count: 3000, stream: 2), second = Self.points(in: region, count: 3000, stream: 3)
        let (a, b) = (solid.signedDistances(at: first), solid.signedDistances(at: second))
        var worst = 0.0
        for index in first.indices {
            let gap = (first[index] - second[index]).length
            guard gap > 1e-9 else { continue }
            worst = max(worst, abs(a[index] - b[index]) / gap)
        }
        #expect(worst <= solid.lipschitz + 1e-9, "\(name): measured \(worst) against L = \(solid.lipschitz)")
    }

    @Test func nearbyPairsAreWithinTheConstantToo() throws {
        // Close pairs probe the local gradient, where a missing derivative term (a graded blend) would show.
        for (name, solid) in ImplicitFixtures.all {
            let region = solid.bounds
            let first = Self.points(in: region, count: 2000, stream: 4)
            let nudged = first.map { $0 + Vector3(0.01, -0.02, 0.015) }
            let (a, b) = (solid.signedDistances(at: first), solid.signedDistances(at: nudged))
            let gap = Vector3(0.01, -0.02, 0.015).length
            let worst = zip(a, b).map { abs($0 - $1) / gap }.max() ?? 0
            #expect(worst <= solid.lipschitz + 1e-6, "\(name): measured \(worst) against L = \(solid.lipschitz)")
        }
    }
}
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit

/// One solid of every kind, for the tests that run over all of them.
enum ImplicitFixtures {
    static let sphere = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 10)
    static let box = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(8, 6, 4), cornerRadius: 1.5)
    static let cylinder = ImplicitSolid.cylinder(radius: 5, halfHeight: 7)

    /// 1 + z / 20: between 0.5 and 1.5 for |z| ≤ 10, slope 0.05 per mm.
    static let graded = Grading(field: .add(.constant(1), .linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 20)), range: 0.5...1.5, slope: 0.05)

    static var moved: ImplicitSolid {
        let move = RigidMove(plane: Plane(origin: Vector3(12, -3, 5), normal: Vector3(1, 1, 0.5), xAxis: Vector3(0, 0, 1)))
        return .moved(box, move ?? .identity)
    }

    static var baked: ImplicitSolid {
        let blocks = BlocksFixture.make(origin: Vector3(-16, -16, -16), spacing: 1, blocks: 4) { $0.length - 9 }
        return .baked(blocks, label: "Ball")
    }

    /// Where |p| / 20 is below 0.5, i.e. a ball of radius 10, clipped to ±15.
    static let isosurface = ImplicitSolid.isosurface(
        Grading(field: .radialGradient(center: .zero, radius: 20), range: 0...2, slope: 0.05), level: 0.5, maxSlope: 0.05,
        region: BoundingBox(min: Vector3(-15, -15, -15), max: Vector3(15, 15, 15)))

    static let all: [(name: String, solid: ImplicitSolid)] = [
        ("sphere", sphere), ("box", box), ("cylinder", cylinder), ("moved", moved),
        ("union", .union([sphere, .moved(box, RigidMove(origin: Vector3(9, 0, 0), xAxis: .unitX, yAxis: .unitY, zAxis: .unitZ))],
                         blend: .constant(0))),
        ("smooth union", .union([sphere, cylinder], blend: .constant(3))),
        ("graded smooth union", .union([sphere, cylinder], blend: graded)),
        ("subtract", .subtract([sphere, cylinder], blend: .constant(0))),
        ("smooth subtract", .subtract([sphere, cylinder, box], blend: .constant(2))),
        ("intersect", .intersect([sphere, box], blend: .constant(0))),
        ("smooth intersect", .intersect([sphere, box], blend: .constant(2.5))),
        ("offset", .offset(box, distance: .constant(2))),
        ("graded offset", .offset(sphere, distance: graded)),
        ("inward shell", .shell(sphere, thickness: .constant(2), side: .inward)),
        ("outward shell", .shell(box, thickness: .constant(1.5), side: .outward)),
        ("graded centred shell", .shell(sphere, thickness: graded, side: .centred)),
        ("baked", baked), ("isosurface", isosurface),
    ]
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Fields/FieldMath.swift`**

````swift
/// The scalar maths of the field nodes (fields spec §3.2), shared by `FieldSampler` (CreatorNodes) and the implicit evaluator
/// (CreatorImplicit) so both give the same number.
public enum FieldMath {
    /// Remap's maths: the linear map of the from range onto the to range, held to the to range when `clamped`. An empty from range
    /// gives `toLow`.
    public static func remap(_ value: Double, fromLow: Double, fromHigh: Double, toLow: Double, toHigh: Double,
                             clamped: Bool) -> Double {
        guard fromHigh != fromLow else { return toLow }
        let mapped = toLow + (value - fromLow) / (fromHigh - fromLow) * (toHigh - toLow)
        guard clamped else { return mapped }
        return minimum(maximum(mapped, min(toLow, toHigh)), max(toLow, toHigh))
    }

    /// The smaller of two numbers, `.nan` when either is: Swift's `min` returns the other operand for a NaN on one side only.
    public static func minimum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double) -> Double {
        a.isNaN || b.isNaN ? .nan : min(a, b)
    }

    /// The larger of two numbers, `.nan` when either is.
    public static func maximum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double) -> Double {
        a.isNaN || b.isNaN ? .nan : max(a, b)
    }

    /// Clamp's maths: `value` held between `low` and `high`, NaN staying NaN.
    public static func clamp(_ value: Double, low: Double, high: Double) -> Double {
        minimum(maximum(value, low), high)
    }

    /// Smooth Blend's maths: the polynomial smooth minimum over `width`; width 0 is the plain minimum.
    public static func smoothMinimum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double, width: Double) -> Double {
        guard width > 0 else { return minimum(a, b) }
        guard !a.isNaN, !b.isNaN else { return .nan }
        let blend = max(width - abs(a - b), 0) / width
        return min(a, b) - blend * blend * width / 4
    }
}
````

````diff
 ///
 /// A stream is an independent run of draws for the same seed and purpose. Stream 0 starts at the (mixed) seed itself; stream `n`

@@ -39,7 +39,7 @@ struct SeededRandom: Sendable {

         if purpose != .plain { state = Self.mix(state ^ purpose.rawValue) }
         if stream != 0 { state = Self.mix(state &+ UInt64(truncatingIfNeeded: stream) &* Self.step) }
@@ -47,7 +47,7 @@ struct SeededRandom: Sendable {
     }
 
     /// SplitMix64's output function: two xor-shift-multiply rounds and a last xor-shift.

+    public static func mix(_ value: UInt64) -> UInt64 {
         var z = value
         z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) &* 0xBF58_476D_1CE4_E5B9
         z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) &* 0x94D0_49BB_1331_11EB
@@ -55,24 +55,24 @@ struct SeededRandom: Sendable {
     }
 
     /// Draw `index` (counting from 0) of this stream.
-    func value(at index: Int) -> UInt64 {
+    public func value(at index: Int) -> UInt64 {
         Self.mix(start &+ UInt64(truncatingIfNeeded: index &+ 1) &* Self.step)
     }
 
     /// The next draw of this stream.

+    public mutating func next() -> UInt64 {

+    public func unit(at index: Int) -> Double {

+    public func below(_ bound: Int, at index: Int) -> Int {

         return Int(value(at: index).multipliedFullWidth(by: UInt64(bound)).high)
     }
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Evaluation/CPUEvaluation.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// The reference evaluation of an implicit solid, in `Double` (implicit spec §4.1): every node's distance, with fields sampled per
/// point. The Metal path must agree with it to 1e-4 mm. One instance keeps the noise generators and profile measures its fields
/// need, so it is made once per batch and used from one task only.
final class CPUEvaluation {
    private struct NoiseKey: Hashable {
        var seed: Int
        var scale: Double
        var octaves: Int
    }

    private var noises: [NoiseKey: FieldNoise] = [:]
    private var profiles: [Field.DistanceSource: ProfileDistance] = [:]

    // MARK: Solids

    // The value of `solid` at `p`. One branch per case, with no `default`, so a case added later is a compile error here.
    // swiftlint:disable:next cyclomatic_complexity
    func distance(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, at p: Vector3) -> Double {
        switch solid {
        case .sphere(let radius):
            return p.length - radius
        case .box(let half, let cornerRadius):
            let inner = Vector3(abs(p.x) - (half.x - cornerRadius), abs(p.y) - (half.y - cornerRadius),
                                abs(p.z) - (half.z - cornerRadius))
            let outside = Vector3(max(inner.x, 0), max(inner.y, 0), max(inner.z, 0)).length
            return outside + min(max(inner.x, inner.y, inner.z), 0) - cornerRadius
        case .cylinder(let radius, let halfHeight):
            let (across, along) = ((p.x * p.x + p.y * p.y).squareRoot() - radius, abs(p.z) - halfHeight)
            return min(max(across, along), 0) + (max(across, 0) * max(across, 0) + max(along, 0) * max(along, 0)).squareRoot()
        case .moved(let child, let move):
            return distance(child, at: move.toLocal(p))
        case .union(let children, let blend):
            let width = max(0, value(blend, at: p))
            return children.dropFirst().reduce(children.first.map { distance($0, at: p) } ?? .infinity) {
                Self.smoothMinimum($0, distance($1, at: p), width: width)
            }
        case .intersect(let children, let blend):
            let width = max(0, value(blend, at: p))
            return children.dropFirst().reduce(children.first.map { distance($0, at: p) } ?? -.infinity) {
                -Self.smoothMinimum(-$0, -distance($1, at: p), width: width)
            }
        case .subtract(let children, let blend):
            let width = max(0, value(blend, at: p))
            return children.dropFirst().reduce(children.first.map { distance($0, at: p) } ?? .infinity) {
                -Self.smoothMinimum(-$0, distance($1, at: p), width: width)
            }
        case .offset(let child, let amount):
            return distance(child, at: p) - value(amount, at: p)
        case .shell(let child, let thickness, let side):
            let wall = max(0, value(thickness, at: p))
            return abs(distance(child, at: p) - side.centreOffset(thickness: wall)) - wall / 2
        case .baked(let blocks, _):
            return blocks.value(at: p)
        case .isosurface(let grading, let level, let maxSlope, let region):
            return max((value(grading, at: p) - level) / maxSlope, Self.boxDistance(p, region))
        }
    }

    /// The polynomial smooth minimum of `a` and `b` over `width`; 0 is the plain minimum.
    static func smoothMinimum(_ a: Double, _ b: Double, width: Double) -> Double {
        guard width > 0 else { return min(a, b) }
        let blend = max(width - abs(a - b), 0) / width
        return min(a, b) - blend * blend * width / 4
    }

    /// The signed distance to the box: negative inside.
    static func boxDistance(_ p: Vector3, _ box: BoundingBox) -> Double {
        let centre = box.center, half = box.size * 0.5
        let inner = Vector3(abs(p.x - centre.x) - half.x, abs(p.y - centre.y) - half.y, abs(p.z - centre.z) - half.z)
        return Vector3(max(inner.x, 0), max(inner.y, 0), max(inner.z, 0)).length + min(max(inner.x, inner.y, inner.z), 0)
    }

    // MARK: Fields

    /// A graded setting at `p`.
    func value(_ grading: Grading, at p: Vector3) -> Double {
        if let constant = grading.constantValue { return constant }
        return value(grading.field, at: p, in: grading)
    }

    // One branch per field case, with no `default`.
    // swiftlint:disable:next cyclomatic_complexity
    private func value(_ field: Field, at p: Vector3, in grading: Grading) -> Double {
        switch field {
        case .constant(let value):
            return value
        case .distance(let source, let signed):
            switch source {
            case .point(let target):
                return (p - target).length
            case .profile:
                if profiles[source] == nil, case .profile(let profile) = source { profiles[source] = ProfileDistance(profile) }
                return profiles[source]?.distance(from: p) ?? .infinity
            case .kernel(let target):
                return grading.blocks(for: target.identity, signed: signed)?.value(at: p) ?? .nan
            }
        case .linearGradient(let plane, let length):
            return (p - plane.origin).dot(plane.normal.normalized ?? .zero) / length
        case .radialGradient(let center, let radius):
            return (p - center).length / radius
        case .noise(let seed, let scale, let octaves):
            let key = NoiseKey(seed: seed, scale: scale, octaves: octaves)
            if noises[key] == nil { noises[key] = FieldNoise(seed: seed, scale: scale, octaves: octaves) }
            return noises[key]?.value(at: p) ?? 0.5
        case .image(let image, let plane, let width, let height, let edges):
            let across = plane.xAxis.normalized ?? .zero, up = plane.yAxis.normalized ?? .zero
            let offset = p - plane.origin
            return image.value(u: offset.dot(across) / width + 0.5, v: 0.5 - offset.dot(up) / height, edges: edges)
        case .implicit(let reference):
            return reference.solid.map { distance($0, at: p) } ?? reference.payload.signedDistance(at: p)
        case .add(let a, let b):
            return value(a, at: p, in: grading) + value(b, at: p, in: grading)
        case .multiply(let a, let b):
            return value(a, at: p, in: grading) * value(b, at: p, in: grading)
        case .minimum(let a, let b):
            return FieldMath.minimum(value(a, at: p, in: grading), value(b, at: p, in: grading))
        case .maximum(let a, let b):
            return FieldMath.maximum(value(a, at: p, in: grading), value(b, at: p, in: grading))
        case .remap(let inner, let fromLow, let fromHigh, let toLow, let toHigh, let clamped):
            return FieldMath.remap(value(inner, at: p, in: grading), fromLow: fromLow, fromHigh: fromHigh, toLow: toLow,
                                   toHigh: toHigh, clamped: clamped)
        case .clamp(let inner, let low, let high):
            return FieldMath.clamp(value(inner, at: p, in: grading), low: low, high: high)
        case .smoothMinimum(let a, let b, let width):
            return FieldMath.smoothMinimum(value(a, at: p, in: grading), value(b, at: p, in: grading), width: width)
        }
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Evaluation/ImplicitSolid+Evaluation.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph

/// An implicit solid travels along wires and sits in field leaves as an `ImplicitPayload` (CreatorGraph cannot name this type).
extension ImplicitSolid: ImplicitPayload {
    public var summary: String { description }
    public var payloadBounds: BoundingBox { bounds }
}

extension ImplicitSolid {
    /// The signed distance at `point` in millimetres, negative inside: the CPU reference (implicit spec §4.1), in `Double`.
    public func signedDistance(at point: Vector3) -> Double {
        CPUEvaluation().distance(self, at: point)
    }

    /// The signed distance at each of `points`, sharing one evaluation's set-up (noise generators, profile measures).
    public func signedDistances(at points: [Vector3]) -> [Double] {
        let evaluation = CPUEvaluation()
        return points.map { evaluation.distance(self, at: $0) }
    }

    /// This solid as a value on a wire.
    public var reference: ImplicitReference { ImplicitReference(self) }
}

extension ImplicitReference {
    /// The solid behind this reference, when it is an `ImplicitSolid` (always, outside tests).
    public var solid: ImplicitSolid? { payload as? ImplicitSolid }
}
````

````diff
@@ -22,6 +22,10 @@ public final class DistanceBlocks: Hashable, Sendable {
     public let coarse: [Float]
     /// Distances up to this size from the surface are stored exactly; beyond it the value is a bound.
     public let band: Double
+    /// A bound on how fast the interpolated value changes: at least 1, and for a baked distance close to it. Inside one cell of
+    /// samples the trilinear gradient is largest at a corner, so the bound is the largest vector of sample differences over `spacing`
+    /// there, over the cells near the surface.
+    public let lipschitz: Double
 
     public init(origin: Vector3, spacing: Double, blockCounts: [Int], table: [Int32], pool: [Float], coarse: [Float], band: Double) {
         precondition(blockCounts.count == 3 && table.count == blockCounts.reduce(1, *) && coarse.count == table.count,
@@ -33,6 +37,44 @@ public final class DistanceBlocks: Hashable, Sendable {
         self.pool = pool
         self.coarse = coarse
         self.band = band
+        self.lipschitz = Self.interpolationSlope(blockCounts: blockCounts, table: table, pool: pool, coarse: coarse, spacing: spacing,
+                                              band: band)
+    }
+
+    /// The bound `lipschitz` describes. The squared gradient of a trilinear cell is convex in position, so its largest value is at a
+    /// corner, where the gradient is the three sample differences along the edges that meet there. Only cells within half the band of
+    /// the surface count: deeper in, the true distance has ridges (the medial axis) that no pruning decision depends on.
+    private static func interpolationSlope(blockCounts: [Int], table: [Int32], pool: [Float], coarse: [Float], spacing: Double,
+                                           band: Double) -> Double {
+        let size = blockSize
+        let counts = blockCounts.map { $0 * size }
+        func sample(_ i: Int, _ j: Int, _ k: Int) -> Float {
+            let block = ((k / size) * blockCounts[1] + j / size) * blockCounts[0] + i / size
+            let slot = Int(table[block])
+            guard slot >= 0 else { return coarse[block] }
+            return pool[slot * size * size * size + ((k % size) * size + j % size) * size + i % size]
+        }
+        let reach = Float(band / 2)
+        var worst: Float = 0
+        for k in 0..<counts[2] - 1 {
+            for j in 0..<counts[1] - 1 {
+                for i in 0..<counts[0] - 1 {
+                    var nearest = Float.infinity
+                    for corner in 0..<8 {
+                        nearest = min(nearest, abs(sample(i + (corner & 1), j + ((corner >> 1) & 1), k + (corner >> 2))))
+                    }
+                    guard nearest <= reach else { continue }
+                    for corner in 0..<8 {
+                        let (a, b, c) = (corner & 1, (corner >> 1) & 1, corner >> 2)
+                        let dx = sample(i + 1, j + b, k + c) - sample(i, j + b, k + c)
+                        let dy = sample(i + a, j + 1, k + c) - sample(i + a, j, k + c)
+                        let dz = sample(i + a, j + b, k + 1) - sample(i + a, j + b, k)
+                        worst = max(worst, dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return max(1, Double(worst).squareRoot() / spacing)
     }
 
     public static func == (lhs: DistanceBlocks, rhs: DistanceBlocks) -> Bool { lhs === rhs }
````

### Task 4: Field.slopeBound and Field.valueRange

Give every field a conservative bound on how fast it changes (spec §3.3) and, where there is one, the range of its values. The Shell, Offset, blend and Isosurface nodes build their lipschitz constants from them.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGeometry/Fields/FieldNoise.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Field/Field+Slope.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/Support/ImplicitFixtures.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/FieldSlopeTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `Field` and every leaf of it; `GreyImage`; `ImplicitPayload.lipschitz`; `FieldNoise` (Task 3).
- Produces (the public declarations this task adds; later tasks use these exact names):

````swift
public struct FieldSlope: Hashable, Sendable
public var value: Double
public var isKnown: Bool
public init(value: Double, isKnown: Bool = true)
public static let unknownSlope = 10.0
public var slopeBound: FieldSlope
public var valueRange: ClosedRange<Double>?
````


**Notes for the implementer:**

- Noise's bound is *declared*, not proved: `FieldNoise.unitSlope = 4` per octave of size 1 mm, measured at 3.15 over four million random directions and seeds. `FieldSlopeTests` checks every field kind against the sampler on random pairs.
- An unknown bound (a product with a side of no known range, a distance multiplied by a distance) is taken as 10 per mm and flagged: `FieldSlope.isKnown`.

````diff
+    /// Noise remapped to 0.6 ... 1.6 mm, with the slope `Field.slopeBound` declares for it.

+        let field = Field.remap(.noise(seed: 4, scale: 6, octaves: 2), fromLow: 0, fromHigh: 1, toLow: 0.6, toHigh: 1.6, clamped: true)
+        return Grading(field: field, range: 0.6...1.6, slope: field.slopeBound.value)
+    }
+
     static var moved: ImplicitSolid {
         let move = RigidMove(plane: Plane(origin: Vector3(12, -3, 5), normal: Vector3(1, 1, 0.5), xAxis: Vector3(0, 0, 1)))
         return .moved(box, move ?? .identity)
@@ -41,6 +47,8 @@ enum ImplicitFixtures {
         ("inward shell", .shell(sphere, thickness: .constant(2), side: .inward)),
         ("outward shell", .shell(box, thickness: .constant(1.5), side: .outward)),
         ("graded centred shell", .shell(sphere, thickness: graded, side: .centred)),
+        ("noisy shell", .shell(box, thickness: noisy, side: .inward)),
+        ("noisy smooth union", .union([sphere, cylinder], blend: noisy)),
         ("baked", baked), ("isosurface", isosurface),
     ]
 }
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// `Field.slopeBound` and `Field.valueRange` (implicit spec §3.3): hand-worked values, then the bound against the sampler on random
/// pairs of points.
struct FieldSlopeTests {
    struct Ball: ImplicitPayload, Hashable {
        var radius: Double
        var summary: String { "ball" }
        var estimatedBytes: Int { 96 }
        var payloadBounds: BoundingBox { BoundingBox(min: Vector3(-radius, -radius, -radius), max: Vector3(radius, radius, radius)) }
        var lipschitz: Double { 1.5 }
        func signedDistance(at point: Vector3) -> Double { 1.5 * (point.length - radius) }
    }

    /// A 4 × 4 picture whose columns step 0, 21,845, 43,690 and 65,535: a quarter of the range per column.
    static let ramp = GreyImage(width: 4, height: 4,
                                pixels: (0..<16).map { UInt16(($0 % 4) * 21_845) })

    func slope(_ field: Field) -> Double { field.slopeBound.value }

    @Test func sourcesHaveTheirAnalyticSlopes() {
        #expect(slope(.constant(5)) == 0)
        #expect(slope(.distance(.point(.zero), signed: false)) == 1)
        #expect(slope(.linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 20)) == 0.05)
        #expect(slope(.radialGradient(center: .zero, radius: 4)) == 0.25)
        #expect(Field.constant(1).slopeBound.isKnown)
    }

    @Test func noiseHasADeclaredBound() {
        #expect(slope(.noise(seed: 1, scale: 8, octaves: 1)) == FieldNoise.unitSlope / 8)
        // Two octaves: 2 × 4 / (2 × 0.75 × 8).
        #expect(abs(slope(.noise(seed: 1, scale: 8, octaves: 2)) - 8.0 / 12) < 1e-12)
    }

    @Test func anImageIsBoundedByItsPixelDifferences() throws {
        let image = try #require(Self.ramp)
        // Across: 21,845 / 65,535 = 1/3 per pixel; the picture is 8 mm wide, so a pixel is 2 mm: 1/6 per mm. Down: 0.
        let clamped = Field.image(image, plane: .xy, width: 8, height: 8, edges: .clamp)
        #expect(abs(slope(clamped) - 1.0 / 6) < 1e-5)
        // Tiling counts the wrap from the last column back to the first: a step of 65,535, a full unit per pixel.
        let tiled = Field.image(image, plane: .xy, width: 8, height: 8, edges: .tile)
        #expect(abs(slope(tiled) - 0.5) < 1e-5)
    }

    @Test func anImplicitTakesItsPayloadsBound() {
        #expect(slope(.implicit(ImplicitReference(Ball(radius: 3)))) == 1.5)
    }

    @Test func theMathsCombinesBounds() {
        let linear = Field.linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 10)
        let radial = Field.radialGradient(center: .zero, radius: 5)
        #expect(abs(slope(.add(linear, radial)) - 0.3) < 1e-12)
        #expect(slope(.minimum(linear, radial)) == 0.2 && slope(.maximum(linear, radial)) == 0.2)
        #expect(slope(.smoothMinimum(linear, radial, width: 2)) == 0.2)
        #expect(abs(slope(.remap(linear, fromLow: 0, fromHigh: 2, toLow: 0, toHigh: 10, clamped: true)) - 0.5) < 1e-12)
        #expect(slope(.remap(linear, fromLow: 1, fromHigh: 1, toLow: 0, toHigh: 10, clamped: false)) == 0)
        #expect(slope(.clamp(radial, low: 0, high: 1)) == 0.2)
        // Noise is in 0…1: its product with a constant 3 weighs the noise's slope by 3.
        let noise = Field.noise(seed: 1, scale: 8, octaves: 1)
        #expect(abs(slope(.multiply(noise, .constant(3))) - 3 * 0.5) < 1e-12)
        // Two noises: each weighs the other's range (1).
        #expect(abs(slope(.multiply(noise, noise)) - 1.0) < 1e-12)
    }

    @Test func aProductWithAnUnboundedSideHasNoBound() {
        let distance = Field.distance(.point(.zero), signed: false)
        let product = Field.multiply(distance, .linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 10))
        #expect(!product.slopeBound.isKnown && product.slopeBound.value == Field.unknownSlope)
        #expect(Field.add(product, .constant(1)).slopeBound.value >= Field.unknownSlope)
        // Clamping both sides gives each a range again: 1 × slope(distance) + 5 × slope(linear).
        let clamped = Field.multiply(.clamp(distance, low: 0, high: 5), .clamp(.linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 10), low: 0, high: 1))
        #expect(clamped.slopeBound.isKnown && abs(slope(clamped) - (1 + 5 * 0.1)) < 1e-12)
    }

    @Test func rangesFollowTheMaths() {
        let noise = Field.noise(seed: 1, scale: 8, octaves: 2)
        #expect(noise.valueRange == 0...1 && Field.constant(2).valueRange == 2...2)
        #expect(Field.distance(.point(.zero), signed: true).valueRange == nil)
        #expect(Field.add(noise, .constant(1)).valueRange == 1...2)
        #expect(Field.multiply(noise, .constant(-3)).valueRange == -3...0)
        #expect(Field.remap(noise, fromLow: 0, fromHigh: 1, toLow: 0.5, toHigh: 1.5, clamped: false).valueRange == 0.5...1.5)
        #expect(Field.remap(.distance(.point(.zero), signed: false), fromLow: 0, fromHigh: 1, toLow: 2, toHigh: 1, clamped: true)
            .valueRange == 1...2)
        #expect(Field.clamp(.distance(.point(.zero), signed: false), low: 1, high: 3).valueRange == 1...3)
        #expect(Field.minimum(noise, .constant(0.25)).valueRange == 0...0.25)
        #expect(Field.maximum(noise, .constant(0.25)).valueRange == 0.25...1)
        #expect(Field.smoothMinimum(noise, .constant(0.25), width: 1).valueRange == -0.25...0.25)
        #expect(Field.multiply(noise, .linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 3)).valueRange == nil)
    }

    // MARK: the bound against the sampler

    func fields() throws -> [(String, Field)] {
        let image = try #require(Self.ramp)
        return [
            ("linear", .linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 7)),
            ("radial", .radialGradient(center: Vector3(1, 2, 3), radius: 6)),
            ("distance", .distance(.point(Vector3(1, 2, 3)), signed: false)),
            ("noise 1", .noise(seed: 3, scale: 4, octaves: 1)),
            ("noise 3", .noise(seed: 9, scale: 2.5, octaves: 3)),
            ("image tile", .image(image, plane: .xy, width: 6, height: 5, edges: .tile)),
            ("image clamp", .image(image, plane: .xy, width: 6, height: 5, edges: .clamp)),
            ("implicit", .implicit(ImplicitReference(Ball(radius: 3)))),
            ("sum", .add(.noise(seed: 3, scale: 4, octaves: 2), .radialGradient(center: .zero, radius: 5))),
            ("product", .multiply(.noise(seed: 3, scale: 4, octaves: 2),
                                  .clamp(.radialGradient(center: .zero, radius: 5), low: 0, high: 2))),
            ("remap", .remap(.noise(seed: 5, scale: 3, octaves: 2), fromLow: 0.3, fromHigh: 0.7, toLow: 0.5, toHigh: 2, clamped: true)),
            ("smooth min", .smoothMinimum(.noise(seed: 5, scale: 3, octaves: 1), .linearGradient(plane: .xy, length: 9), width: 1)),
        ]
    }

    @Test func theBoundHoldsOnRandomPairs() async throws {
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        for (name, field) in try fields() {
            let random = SeededRandom(seed: 11, stream: 5)
            let points = (0..<1500).map { Vector3(random.unit(at: 3 * $0) * 20 - 10, random.unit(at: 3 * $0 + 1) * 20 - 10,
                                                 random.unit(at: 3 * $0 + 2) * 20 - 10) }
            let nudged = points.enumerated().map { index, point in
                point + Vector3(random.unit(at: 5000 + 3 * index) - 0.5, random.unit(at: 5001 + 3 * index) - 0.5,
                                random.unit(at: 5002 + 3 * index) - 0.5) * 0.4
            }
            let (a, b) = (try await FieldSampler.sample(field, at: points, kernel: kernel),
                          try await FieldSampler.sample(field, at: nudged, kernel: kernel))
            var worst = 0.0
            for index in points.indices {
                let gap = (points[index] - nudged[index]).length
                if gap > 1e-9 { worst = max(worst, abs(a[index] - b[index]) / gap) }
            }
            #expect(worst <= field.slopeBound.value + 1e-9, "\(name): measured \(worst) against \(field.slopeBound.value)")
        }
    }

    @Test func rangesContainTheSamples() async throws {
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        let random = SeededRandom(seed: 12, stream: 5)
        let points = (0..<2000).map { Vector3(random.unit(at: 3 * $0) * 30 - 15, random.unit(at: 3 * $0 + 1) * 30 - 15,
                                             random.unit(at: 3 * $0 + 2) * 30 - 15) }
        for (name, field) in try fields() {
            guard let range = field.valueRange else { continue }
            let values = try await FieldSampler.sample(field, at: points, kernel: kernel)
            #expect(values.allSatisfy { range.contains($0) }, "\(name): \(values.min() ?? 0)...\(values.max() ?? 0) outside \(range)")
        }
    }
}
````
