````swift
/// Which GPU engine this platform has. The only place outside the engines themselves that knows a platform's GPU API, and it names no
/// type of one: it only asks `canImport`.
public enum ImplicitGPUEngines {
    /// The engine for this machine's GPU, or `nil` where there is none (no device, or a platform with no engine yet).
    public static var platformDefault: (any ImplicitGPUEngine)? {
        #if canImport(Metal)
        MetalImplicitEngine.shared
        #else
        nil
        #endif
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/CompiledImplicit.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

/// An implicit solid turned into Metal (implicit spec §4.1): the source of one `float sdf(...)` function and the kernel that calls it
/// over a batch of points, and the buffers it reads. The source depends on the tree's *shape* only (node kinds, wiring and which
/// settings are fields), so a pipeline compiled for one solid serves every solid of that shape; the numbers are in the buffers.
public struct CompiledImplicit: Sendable {
    /// Names this compiled solid's uploaded buffers.
    public let id = UUID()
    /// The complete Metal source: `ImplicitShaderLibrary`, the generated `sdf` and the kernel `evaluateImplicit`.
    public let source: String
    /// Float parameters (positions, sizes, radii, thresholds).
    public let parameters: [Float]
    /// Integer parameters (sides, flags, octave counts, offsets and sizes into the other buffers).
    public let integers: [Int32]
    /// Block tables of baked distances, and noise seeds.
    public let tables: [Int32]
    /// Block samples and the bounds of unstored blocks.
    public let pool: [Float]
    /// Picture pixels, 0 to 65,535.
    public let images: [Float]

    /// The name of the kernel in `source`.
    public static let kernelName = "evaluateImplicit"
}

/// Why a solid can't run on the GPU.
public enum ImplicitCompileError: Error, Equatable, Sendable {
    /// Some part of the tree has no Metal form; the evaluation runs on the CPU instead.
    case unsupported(String)
    /// Metal refused the generated source, which is a bug here; the text is Metal's.
    case metal(String)
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/ImplicitCompiler.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Generates Metal for an `ImplicitSolid` (implicit spec §4.1): one statement per node of the tree, in evaluation order, each a port of
/// the matching branch of `CPUEvaluation`. Numbers go to the parameter buffers and the source names their slots, so the source is the
/// same for every solid of one shape.
public enum ImplicitCompiler {
    public static func compile(_ solid: ImplicitSolid) throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> CompiledImplicit {
        var generator = Generator()
        let result = try generator.solid(solid, at: "p")
        let source = ImplicitShaderLibrary.source + """


        inline float sdf(const thread Env& e, float3 p) {
        \(generator.lines.joined(separator: "\n"))
            return \(result);
        }

        kernel void \(CompiledImplicit.kernelName)(device const packed_float3* points [[buffer(0)]], device float* out [[buffer(1)]],
                                                   device const float* P [[buffer(2)]], device const int* I [[buffer(3)]],
                                                   device const int* tables [[buffer(4)]], device const float* pool [[buffer(5)]],
                                                   device const float* images [[buffer(6)]], constant uint& count [[buffer(7)]],
                                                   uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {
            if (gid >= count) { return; }
            Env e = { P, I, tables, pool, images };
            out[gid] = sdf(e, float3(points[gid]));
        }
        """
        return CompiledImplicit(source: source, parameters: generator.parameters, integers: generator.integers,
                                tables: generator.tables, pool: generator.pool, images: generator.images)
    }

    /// The generator's state: statements so far and the buffers they read.
    private struct Generator {
        var lines: [String] = []
        var parameters: [Float] = []
        var integers: [Int32] = []
        var tables: [Int32] = []
        var pool: [Float] = []
        var images: [Float] = []
        var variables = 0
        var registered: [ObjectIdentifier: (integers: Int, parameters: Int)] = [:]

        // MARK: Pieces

        mutating func declare(_ expression: String) -> String {
            variables += 1
            lines.append("    float v\(variables) = \(expression);")
            return "v\(variables)"
        }

        mutating func declareVector(_ expression: String) -> String {
            variables += 1
            lines.append("    float3 v\(variables) = \(expression);")
            return "v\(variables)"
        }

        /// A float parameter's name in the shader.
        mutating func number(_ value: Double) -> String {
            parameters.append(Float(value))
            return "e.P[\(parameters.count - 1)]"
        }

        /// An integer parameter's name in the shader.
        mutating func integer(_ value: Int) -> String {
            integers.append(Int32(truncatingIfNeeded: value))
            return "e.I[\(integers.count - 1)]"
        }

        mutating func vector(_ value: Vector3) -> String {
            "float3(\(number(value.x)), \(number(value.y)), \(number(value.z)))"
        }

        /// Stores baked distance blocks (once, however often they are used) and returns where their parameters are.
        mutating func register(_ blocks: DistanceBlocks) -> (integers: Int, parameters: Int) {
            if let known = registered[ObjectIdentifier(blocks)] { return known }
            let slot = (integers: integers.count, parameters: parameters.count)
            integers += [Int32(tables.count), Int32(pool.count), Int32(pool.count + blocks.pool.count),
                         Int32(blocks.blockCounts[0]), Int32(blocks.blockCounts[1]), Int32(blocks.blockCounts[2]),
            ]
            tables += blocks.table
            pool += blocks.pool
            pool += blocks.coarse
            parameters += [Float(blocks.origin.x), Float(blocks.origin.y), Float(blocks.origin.z), Float(blocks.spacing)]
            registered[ObjectIdentifier(blocks)] = slot
            return slot
        }

        // MARK: Solids

        // One branch per case, with no `default`, so a case added later is a compile error here.
        // swiftlint:disable:next cyclomatic_complexity
        mutating func solid(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, at p: String) throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> String {
            switch solid {
            case .sphere(let radius):
                return declare("length(\(p)) - \(number(radius))")
            case .box(let half, let cornerRadius):
                return declare("sdBox(\(p), \(vector(half)), \(number(cornerRadius)))")
            case .cylinder(let radius, let halfHeight):
                return declare("sdCylinder(\(p), \(number(radius)), \(number(halfHeight)))")
            case .moved(let child, let move):
                let (origin, x, y, z) = (vector(move.origin), vector(move.xAxis), vector(move.yAxis), vector(move.zAxis))
                let local = declareVector("float3(dot(\(p) - \(origin), \(x)), dot(\(p) - \(origin), \(y)), dot(\(p) - \(origin), \(z)))")
                return try self.solid(child, at: local)
            case .union(let children, let blend):
                return try combine(children, blend, at: p, join: { "smoothMin(\($0), \($1), \($2))" })
            case .intersect(let children, let blend):
                return try combine(children, blend, at: p, join: { "-smoothMin(-\($0), -\($1), \($2))" })
            case .subtract(let children, let blend):
                return try combine(children, blend, at: p, join: { "-smoothMin(-\($0), \($1), \($2))" })
            case .offset(let child, let distance):
                let inner = try self.solid(child, at: p)
                return declare("\(inner) - \(try grading(distance, at: p))")
            case .shell(let child, let thickness, let side):
                let inner = try self.solid(child, at: p)
                let wall = declare("max(0.0, \(try grading(thickness, at: p)))")
                let side = integer(side.rawValue)
                let centre = declare("(\(side) == 0 ? -\(wall) / 2.0 : (\(side) == 1 ? \(wall) / 2.0 : 0.0))")
                return declare("abs(\(inner) - \(centre)) - \(wall) / 2.0")
            case .baked(let blocks, _):
                let slot = register(blocks)
                return declare("bakedValue(e, \(slot.integers), \(slot.parameters), \(p))")
            case .isosurface(let graded, let level, let maxSlope, let region):
                let value = try grading(graded, at: p)
                let (levelName, slopeName) = (number(level), number(maxSlope))
                let (centre, half) = (vector(region.center), vector(region.size * 0.5))
                return declare("max((\(value) - \(levelName)) / \(slopeName), regionDistance(\(p), \(centre), \(half)))")
            }
        }

        /// Union, intersect and subtract: the children folded left to right with `join(result, next, width)`.
        mutating func combine(_ children: [ImplicitSolid], _ blend: Grading, at p: String,
                              join: (String, String, String) -> String) throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> String {
            let width = declare("max(0.0, \(try grading(blend, at: p)))")
            var names: [String] = []
            for child in children { names.append(try solid(child, at: p)) }
            guard var result = names.first else { return declare("1e30") }
            for name in names.dropFirst() { result = declare(join(result, name, width)) }
            return result
        }

        // MARK: Fields

        mutating func grading(_ grading: Grading, at p: String) throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> String {
            if let constant = grading.constantValue { return number(constant) }
            return try field(grading.field, at: p, in: grading)
        }

        // One branch per field case, with no `default`.
        // swiftlint:disable:next cyclomatic_complexity function_body_length
        mutating func field(_ field: Field, at p: String, in grading: Grading) throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> String {
            switch field {
            case .constant(let value):
                return number(value)
            case .distance(let source, let signed):
                switch source {
                case .point(let target):
                    return declare("length(\(p) - \(vector(target)))")
                case .profile:
                    throw .unsupported("a distance to a profile")
                case .kernel(let target):
                    guard let blocks = grading.blocks(for: target.identity, signed: signed) else {
                        throw .unsupported("a distance to a solid that was not baked")
                    }
                    let slot = register(blocks)
                    return declare("bakedValue(e, \(slot.integers), \(slot.parameters), \(p))")
                }
            case .linearGradient(let plane, let length):
                let (origin, normal) = (vector(plane.origin), vector(plane.normal.normalized ?? .zero))
                return declare("dot(\(p) - \(origin), \(normal)) / \(number(length))")
            case .radialGradient(let center, let radius):
                return declare("length(\(p) - \(vector(center))) / \(number(radius))")
            case .noise(let seed, let scale, let octaves):
                let seeds = tables.count
                for octave in 0..<octaves {
                    let start = SeededRandom(seed: seed, stream: octave, purpose: .fieldNoise).streamStart
                    tables += [Int32(truncatingIfNeeded: start & 0xFFFF_FFFF), Int32(truncatingIfNeeded: start >> 32)]
                }
                let (scaleName, octavesName, seedsName) = (number(scale), integer(octaves), integer(seeds))
                return declare("fieldNoise(e, \(p), \(scaleName), \(octavesName), \(seedsName))")
            case .image(let image, let plane, let width, let height, let edges):
                let slot = (integers: integers.count, parameters: parameters.count)
                integers += [Int32(images.count), Int32(image.width), Int32(image.height), Int32(edges.rawValue)]
                images += image.pixels.map(Float.init)
                let (origin, across, up) = (vector(plane.origin), vector(plane.xAxis.normalized ?? .zero),
                                            vector(plane.yAxis.normalized ?? .zero))
                let (widthName, heightName) = (number(width), number(height))
                let offset = declareVector("\(p) - \(origin)")
                return declare("imageValue(e, \(slot.integers), dot(\(offset), \(across)) / \(widthName) + 0.5, "
                    + "0.5 - dot(\(offset), \(up)) / \(heightName))")
            case .implicit(let reference):
                guard let solid = reference.solid else { throw .unsupported("an implicit that is not an ImplicitSolid") }
                return try self.solid(solid, at: p)
            case .add(let a, let b):
                return declare("\(try self.field(a, at: p, in: grading)) + \(try self.field(b, at: p, in: grading))")
            case .multiply(let a, let b):
                return declare("\(try self.field(a, at: p, in: grading)) * \(try self.field(b, at: p, in: grading))")
            case .minimum(let a, let b):
                return declare("min(\(try self.field(a, at: p, in: grading)), \(try self.field(b, at: p, in: grading)))")
            case .maximum(let a, let b):
                return declare("max(\(try self.field(a, at: p, in: grading)), \(try self.field(b, at: p, in: grading)))")
            case .remap(let inner, let fromLow, let fromHigh, let toLow, let toHigh, let clamped):
                let value = try self.field(inner, at: p, in: grading)
                let numbers = [fromLow, fromHigh, toLow, toHigh].map { number($0) }
                return declare("remapField(\(value), \(numbers.joined(separator: ", ")), \(integer(clamped ? 1 : 0)))")
            case .clamp(let inner, let low, let high):
                let value = try self.field(inner, at: p, in: grading)
                return declare("min(max(\(value), \(number(low))), \(number(high)))")
            case .smoothMinimum(let a, let b, let width):
                let (x, y) = (try self.field(a, at: p, in: grading), try self.field(b, at: p, in: grading))
                return declare("smoothMin(\(x), \(y), \(number(width)))")
            }
        }
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/ImplicitShaderLibrary.swift`**

````swift
/// The Metal Shading Language every compiled implicit shares (implicit spec §4.1): the distance functions of the primitives, the smooth
/// minimum, the lookup into baked distance blocks, simplex noise and picture sampling. Each is a line-for-line port of its CPU twin
/// (`CPUEvaluation`, `DistanceBlocks.value(at:)`, `FieldNoise`, `GreyImage.value`), and the agreement suite holds the two to 1e-4 mm.
///
/// Data reaches a shader through five buffers: `P` (float parameters), `I` (integer parameters), `tables` (block tables and noise
/// seeds), `pool` (block samples) and `images` (pixels). A compiled tree's source names slots in them but never holds a number, so
/// editing a value changes the buffers and not the source.
enum ImplicitShaderLibrary {
    static let source = """
    #include <metal_stdlib>
    using namespace metal;

    struct Env {
        device const float* P;
        device const int* I;
        device const int* tables;
        device const float* pool;
        device const float* images;
    };

    inline float smoothMin(float a, float b, float k) {
        if (!(k > 0.0)) { return min(a, b); }
        float blend = max(k - abs(a - b), 0.0) / k;
        return min(a, b) - blend * blend * k * 0.25;
    }

    inline float sdBox(float3 p, float3 halfSize, float r) {
        float3 q = abs(p) - (halfSize - r);
        return length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, max(q.y, q.z)), 0.0) - r;
    }

    inline float regionDistance(float3 p, float3 centre, float3 halfSize) {
        float3 q = abs(p - centre) - halfSize;
        return length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, max(q.y, q.z)), 0.0);
    }

    inline float sdCylinder(float3 p, float r, float halfHeight) {
        float across = length(p.xy) - r;
        float along = abs(p.z) - halfHeight;
        return min(max(across, along), 0.0) + length(max(float2(across, along), 0.0));
    }

    // MARK: baked distance blocks (DistanceBlocks)

    inline float bakedSample(const thread Env& e, int ioff, int i, int j, int k) {
        int nx = e.I[ioff + 3], ny = e.I[ioff + 4], nz = e.I[ioff + 5];
        i = clamp(i, 0, nx * 8 - 1);
        j = clamp(j, 0, ny * 8 - 1);
        k = clamp(k, 0, nz * 8 - 1);
        int block = ((k / 8) * ny + (j / 8)) * nx + (i / 8);
        int slot = e.tables[e.I[ioff] + block];
        if (slot < 0) { return e.pool[e.I[ioff + 2] + block]; }
        return e.pool[e.I[ioff + 1] + slot * 512 + ((k % 8) * 8 + (j % 8)) * 8 + (i % 8)];
    }

    inline float bakedValue(const thread Env& e, int ioff, int poff, float3 point) {
        float3 origin = float3(e.P[poff], e.P[poff + 1], e.P[poff + 2]);
        float spacing = e.P[poff + 3];
        float3 last = float3(e.I[ioff + 3] * 8 - 1, e.I[ioff + 4] * 8 - 1, e.I[ioff + 5] * 8 - 1);
        float3 held = clamp(point, origin, origin + last * spacing);
        float outside = length(point - held);
        float3 grid = clamp((held - origin) / spacing, float3(0.0), last);
        int ix = int(floor(grid.x)), iy = int(floor(grid.y)), iz = int(floor(grid.z));
        float tx = grid.x - float(ix), ty = grid.y - float(iy), tz = grid.z - float(iz);
        float x00 = mix(bakedSample(e, ioff, ix, iy, iz), bakedSample(e, ioff, ix + 1, iy, iz), tx);
        float x10 = mix(bakedSample(e, ioff, ix, iy + 1, iz), bakedSample(e, ioff, ix + 1, iy + 1, iz), tx);
        float x01 = mix(bakedSample(e, ioff, ix, iy, iz + 1), bakedSample(e, ioff, ix + 1, iy, iz + 1), tx);
        float x11 = mix(bakedSample(e, ioff, ix, iy + 1, iz + 1), bakedSample(e, ioff, ix + 1, iy + 1, iz + 1), tx);
        return mix(mix(x00, x10, ty), mix(x01, x11, ty), tz) + outside;
    }

    // MARK: field maths

    inline float remapField(float x, float fromLow, float fromHigh, float toLow, float toHigh, int clamped) {
        if (fromHigh == fromLow) { return toLow; }
        float mapped = toLow + (x - fromLow) / (fromHigh - fromLow) * (toHigh - toLow);
        if (clamped == 0) { return mapped; }
        return min(max(mapped, min(toLow, toHigh)), max(toLow, toHigh));
    }

    // MARK: simplex noise (FieldNoise)

    constant float3 noiseGradients[12] = {
        float3(1, 1, 0), float3(-1, 1, 0), float3(1, -1, 0), float3(-1, -1, 0), float3(1, 0, 1), float3(-1, 0, 1),
        float3(1, 0, -1), float3(-1, 0, -1), float3(0, 1, 1), float3(0, -1, 1), float3(0, 1, -1), float3(0, -1, -1)
    };

    constant int3 noiseCorners[24] = {
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(1, 0, 0), int3(1, 1, 0), int3(1, 1, 1),
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(1, 0, 0), int3(1, 0, 1), int3(1, 1, 1),
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(0, 0, 1), int3(1, 0, 1), int3(1, 1, 1),
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(0, 0, 1), int3(0, 1, 1), int3(1, 1, 1),
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(0, 1, 0), int3(0, 1, 1), int3(1, 1, 1),
        int3(0, 0, 0), int3(0, 1, 0), int3(1, 1, 0), int3(1, 1, 1)
    };

    inline ulong mix64(ulong value) {
        ulong z = value;
        z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9UL;
        z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBUL;
        return z ^ (z >> 31);
    }

    inline float3 noiseGradient(int i, int j, int k, ulong start) {
        ulong packed = (ulong(i & 0x1FFFFF) << 42) | (ulong(j & 0x1FFFFF) << 21) | ulong(k & 0x1FFFFF);
        ulong draw = mix64(start + (packed + 1) * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15UL);
        return noiseGradients[uint(draw >> 33) % 12];
    }

    inline float simplexOctave(float x, float y, float z, ulong start) {
        float skewed = (x + y + z) * (1.0 / 3.0);
        int i = int(floor(x + skewed)), j = int(floor(y + skewed)), k = int(floor(z + skewed));
        float back = float(i + j + k) * (1.0 / 6.0);
        float x0 = x - (float(i) - back), y0 = y - (float(j) - back), z0 = z - (float(k) - back);
        int ordering;
        if (x0 >= y0) {
            ordering = y0 >= z0 ? 0 : (x0 >= z0 ? 1 : 2);
        } else {
            ordering = y0 < z0 ? 3 : (x0 < z0 ? 4 : 5);
        }
        float sum = 0.0;
        for (int step = 0; step < 4; step++) {
            int3 corner = noiseCorners[ordering * 4 + step];
            float shift = float(step) * (1.0 / 6.0);
            float dx = x0 - float(corner.x) + shift, dy = y0 - float(corner.y) + shift, dz = z0 - float(corner.z) + shift;
            float falloff = 0.6 - dx * dx - dy * dy - dz * dz;
            if (falloff <= 0.0) { continue; }
            float3 gradient = noiseGradient(i + corner.x, j + corner.y, k + corner.z, start);
            float weight = falloff * falloff;
            sum += weight * weight * dot(gradient, float3(dx, dy, dz));
        }
        return 32.0 * sum;
    }

    inline float fieldNoise(const thread Env& e, float3 p, float scale, int octaves, int seeds) {
        float sum = 0.0, total = 0.0, amplitude = 1.0, size = scale;
        for (int octave = 0; octave < octaves; octave++) {
            ulong start = ulong(uint(e.tables[seeds + 2 * octave])) | (ulong(uint(e.tables[seeds + 2 * octave + 1])) << 32);
            sum += amplitude * simplexOctave(p.x / size, p.y / size, p.z / size, start);
            total += amplitude;
            amplitude *= 0.5;
            size *= 0.5;
        }
        return clamp((sum / total + 1.0) / 2.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    }

    // MARK: pictures (GreyImage)

    inline void imageTaps(float position, int count, int edges, thread int& low, thread int& high, thread float& fraction) {
        float size = float(count);
        float held;
        if (edges == 0) {
            float folded = position - floor(position / size) * size;
            held = min(max(folded, 0.0), as_type<float>(as_type<uint>(size) - 1u));
            low = int(floor(held));
            high = (low + 1) % count;
        } else {
            held = min(max(position, 0.0), size - 1.0);
            low = int(floor(held));
            high = min(low + 1, count - 1);
        }
        fraction = held - float(low);
    }

    inline float imageValue(const thread Env& e, int ioff, float u, float v) {
        int offset = e.I[ioff], width = e.I[ioff + 1], height = e.I[ioff + 2], edges = e.I[ioff + 3];
        float x = u * float(width) - 0.5, y = v * float(height) - 0.5;
        if (!isfinite(x) || !isfinite(y)) { return 0.0; }
        int cl, ch, rl, rh;
        float cf, rf;
        imageTaps(x, width, edges, cl, ch, cf);
        imageTaps(y, height, edges, rl, rh, rf);
        float top = mix(e.images[offset + rl * width + cl], e.images[offset + rl * width + ch], cf);
        float bottom = mix(e.images[offset + rh * width + cl], e.images[offset + rh * width + ch], cf);
        return mix(top, bottom, rf) / 65535.0;
    }
    """
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/MetalImplicitEngine.swift`**

````swift
#if canImport(Metal)
import Foundation
@preconcurrency import Metal

/// Runs compiled implicits on the GPU (implicit spec §4.1). One engine per device: it compiles each *shape* once (the pipeline cache is
/// keyed by the generated source) and keeps the buffers of the few compiled solids most recently used, so meshing batch after batch
/// uploads a solid's tables once. The only file that imports Metal (Errata D11), compiled only where Metal exists.
public actor MetalImplicitEngine: ImplicitGPUEngine {
    /// The engine for the system's GPU, or `nil` on a machine with none.
    public static let shared: MetalImplicitEngine? = MetalImplicitEngine()

    /// A solid's uploaded parameters.
    private struct Prepared {
        var parameters: any MTLBuffer
        var integers: any MTLBuffer
        var tables: any MTLBuffer
        var pool: any MTLBuffer
        var images: any MTLBuffer
        var used: Int
    }

    private let device: any MTLDevice
    private let queue: any MTLCommandQueue
    private var pipelines: [String: any MTLComputePipelineState] = [:]
    private var prepared: [UUID: Prepared] = [:]
    private var clock = 0

    /// How many compiled solids keep their buffers.
    private static let preparedLimit = 8
    /// The most points one dispatch takes (12 bytes in, 4 out each).
    static let pointsPerDispatch = 1 << 22

    /// How many pipelines were compiled (a cache hit doesn't count): the tests use it to see that shapes are shared.
    public private(set) var compileCount = 0

    public nonisolated let name = "Metal"

    /// Bumped by `cancel()`: a batch that started before it ends at its next dispatch.
    private var epoch = 0

    public init?() {
        guard let device = MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice(), let queue = device.makeCommandQueue() else { return nil }
        self.device = device
        self.queue = queue
    }

    public var deviceName: String { device.name }

    /// Compiles `program`'s source if no solid of its shape has been.
    public func prepare(_ program: CompiledImplicit) async throws(ImplicitCompileError) {
        _ = try await pipeline(for: program)
    }

    private func pipeline(for program: CompiledImplicit) async throws(ImplicitCompileError) -> any MTLComputePipelineState {
        if let known = pipelines[program.source] { return known }
        do {
            let library = try await device.makeLibrary(source: program.source, options: nil)
            guard let function = library.makeFunction(name: CompiledImplicit.kernelName) else {
                throw ImplicitCompileError.metal("the kernel \(CompiledImplicit.kernelName) is missing")
            }
            let pipeline = try await device.makeComputePipelineState(function: function)
            compileCount += 1
            // A second compile of the same shape that finished first is the one kept.
            if let raced = pipelines[program.source] { return raced }
            pipelines[program.source] = pipeline
            return pipeline
        } catch let error as ImplicitCompileError {
            throw error
        } catch {
            throw .metal(error.localizedDescription)
        }
    }

    /// The value of `program` at each of `points`.
    public func evaluate(_ program: CompiledImplicit, at points: [SIMD3<Float>]) async throws -> [Float] {
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        guard !points.isEmpty else { return [] }
        let started = epoch
        let pipeline = try await pipeline(for: program)
        let buffers = try buffers(for: program)
        var values: [Float] = []
        values.reserveCapacity(points.count)
        for start in stride(from: 0, to: points.count, by: Self.pointsPerDispatch) {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            if epoch != started { throw CancellationError() }
            let slice = points[start..<min(start + Self.pointsPerDispatch, points.count)]
            values += try await dispatch(pipeline, buffers, slice)
        }
        return values
    }

    /// Ends the batches in flight; the next call to `evaluate` runs.
    public func cancel() {
        epoch += 1
    }

    private func dispatch(_ pipeline: any MTLComputePipelineState, _ buffers: Prepared,
                          _ points: ArraySlice<SIMD3<Float>>) async throws -> [Float] {
        let count = points.count
        // The shader reads packed_float3: 12 bytes a point, where SIMD3<Float> is 16.
        var flat: [Float] = []
        flat.reserveCapacity(count * 3)
        for point in points { flat += [point.x, point.y, point.z] }
        guard let input = device.makeBuffer(bytes: flat, length: flat.count * MemoryLayout<Float>.stride, options: .storageModeShared),
              let output = device.makeBuffer(length: count * MemoryLayout<Float>.stride, options: .storageModeShared),
              let commands = queue.makeCommandBuffer(), let encoder = commands.makeComputeCommandEncoder() else {
            throw ImplicitCompileError.metal("a GPU buffer could not be made")
        }
        var total = UInt32(count)
        encoder.setComputePipelineState(pipeline)
        encoder.setBuffer(input, offset: 0, index: 0)
        encoder.setBuffer(output, offset: 0, index: 1)
        encoder.setBuffer(buffers.parameters, offset: 0, index: 2)
        encoder.setBuffer(buffers.integers, offset: 0, index: 3)
        encoder.setBuffer(buffers.tables, offset: 0, index: 4)
        encoder.setBuffer(buffers.pool, offset: 0, index: 5)
        encoder.setBuffer(buffers.images, offset: 0, index: 6)
        encoder.setBytes(&total, length: MemoryLayout<UInt32>.size, index: 7)
        let width = min(pipeline.maxTotalThreadsPerThreadgroup, 256)
        encoder.dispatchThreads(MTLSize(width: count, height: 1, depth: 1),
                                threadsPerThreadgroup: MTLSize(width: width, height: 1, depth: 1))
        encoder.endEncoding()
        await withCheckedContinuation { continuation in
            commands.addCompletedHandler { _ in continuation.resume() }
            commands.commit()
        }
        if let error = commands.error { throw ImplicitCompileError.metal(error.localizedDescription) }
        return Array(UnsafeBufferPointer(start: output.contents().bindMemory(to: Float.self, capacity: count), count: count))
    }

    /// The solid's buffers, uploaded on first use; the least recently used solid's are dropped past `preparedLimit`.
    private func buffers(for program: CompiledImplicit) throws -> Prepared {
        clock += 1
        if var known = prepared[program.id] {
            known.used = clock
            prepared[program.id] = known
            return known
        }
        func upload<T>(_ values: [T]) throws -> any MTLBuffer {
            // Metal won't make an empty buffer: a kind of data this shape doesn't use gets four bytes.
            guard let buffer = device.makeBuffer(length: max(values.count * MemoryLayout<T>.stride, 4), options: .storageModeShared)
            else { throw ImplicitCompileError.metal("a GPU buffer could not be made") }
            values.withUnsafeBytes { bytes in
                if let base = bytes.baseAddress { buffer.contents().copyMemory(from: base, byteCount: bytes.count) }
            }
            return buffer
        }
        let entry = Prepared(parameters: try upload(program.parameters), integers: try upload(program.integers),
                             tables: try upload(program.tables), pool: try upload(program.pool), images: try upload(program.images),
                             used: clock)
        prepared[program.id] = entry
        if prepared.count > Self.preparedLimit, let oldest = prepared.min(by: { $0.value.used < $1.value.used })?.key {
            prepared[oldest] = nil
        }
        return entry
    }
}
#endif
````

### Task 9: Engine selection and the evaluator's mesh context

Choose between a GPU engine and the CPU per solid (`ImplicitEvaluators.make`, in `GPU/`, which names no GPU API and has no logger), and carry the mesh quality, the engine and a progress sink from the app to every node through `EvalContext` (spec §4.3 "EvaluationContext.meshQuality preview/export").

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/DocumentModel.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/EvalContext.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Evaluator+Running.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Evaluator.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Groups/EvaluationSetup.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Implicit/ImplicitEngineChoice.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Implicit/MeshProgress.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/NodeDefinition.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/GPU/ImplicitEvaluators.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/CompiledImplicit.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Metal/MetalImplicitEngine.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Implicit/MeshContextTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/MeshContextProbeNodes.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ImplicitEvaluatorsTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `ImplicitGPUEngine`, `ImplicitGPUEngines`, `GPUBatchEvaluator`, `ImplicitCompiler` (Task 8); `Evaluator`, `EvalContext`, `NodeDefinition`, `DocumentModel` on master.
- Produces (the public declarations this task adds; later tasks use these exact names):

````swift
public enum ImplicitEngineChoice: String, Sendable, Hashable
public struct MeshProgress: Sendable
public init(_ report: @escaping @Sendable (Double) -> Void)
public static let none = MeshProgress
public func callAsFunction(_ fraction: Double)
public enum ImplicitEvaluators
public struct Selection: Sendable
public var evaluator: any ImplicitBatchEvaluator
public var warning: String?
public var diagnostic: String?
public static let cpuWarning = "Running on the CPU"
public static func make(for solid: ImplicitSolid, choice: ImplicitEngineChoice,
````


**Notes for the implementer:**

- A node that reads `context.meshQuality` declares `static let readsMeshQuality = true`; the evaluator then puts the quality in its cache key, so the viewport's preview and an export never share a result. Nodes downstream carry it through their upstream keys.
- `ImplicitEngineChoice.cpu` is what a context made by hand uses, so tests never depend on a GPU. The app passes `.automatic` (Task 14). `make` takes the engine as a parameter (the machine's by default), which is how the selection is tested on every platform with `StubGPUEngine`.

**Create `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Implicit/MeshContextTests.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation
import Synchronization
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph
@testable import CreatorKernel

/// `EvalContext.meshQuality`, `implicitEngine` and `meshProgress` (implicit spec §4.3, §5.3), and what they do to the cache.
struct MeshContextTests {
    func evaluator(_ engine: ImplicitEngineChoice = .cpu) -> Evaluator {
        Evaluator(registry: meshProbeRegistry, kernel: FakeKernel(), implicitEngine: engine)
    }

    /// The integer output `socket` of `node` in `report`.
    func integer(_ report: EvaluationReport, _ node: Node, _ socket: SocketName) -> Int? {
        if case .one(.integer(let value))? = report.results[node.id]?.outputs?[socket] { value } else { nil }
    }

    @Test func aContextMadeByHandPreviewsOnTheCPUAndIgnoresProgress() {
        let context = EvalContext(node: makeNode(PlainProbeNode.self), item: 0, parameters: [:])
        #expect(context.meshQuality == .preview && context.implicitEngine == .cpu)
        context.meshProgress(0.5)
    }

    @Test func theEvaluatorHandsTheQualityAndTheEngineToEveryNode() async throws {
        let probe = makeNode(QualityProbeNode.self, output: true)
        let graph = graph([probe])
        let preview = try await evaluator().evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id])
        #expect(integer(preview, probe, "quality") == 0)
        let export = try await evaluator(.automatic).evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id], meshQuality: .export)
        #expect(integer(export, probe, "quality") == 1)
        #expect(integer(export, probe, "engine") == 1)
        #expect(integer(preview, probe, "engine") == 0)
    }

    @Test func aNodeThatReadsTheQualityDoesNotShareAResultAcrossQualities() async throws {
        let probe = makeNode(QualityProbeNode.self, output: true)
        let graph = graph([probe])
        let one = evaluator()
        let first = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id])
        #expect(first.evaluatedNodes == [probe.id])
        let again = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id])
        #expect(again.evaluatedNodes.isEmpty, "the same quality is cached")
        let export = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id], meshQuality: .export)
        #expect(export.evaluatedNodes == [probe.id], "another quality runs it again")
        #expect(integer(export, probe, "quality") == 1)
        let back = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id])
        #expect(integer(back, probe, "quality") == 0)
    }

    @Test func aNodeThatDoesNotReadTheQualityKeepsItsCachedResult() async throws {
        let probe = makeNode(PlainProbeNode.self, output: true)
        let graph = graph([probe])
        let one = evaluator()
        _ = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id])
        let export = try await one.evaluate(graph, demand: [probe.id], meshQuality: .export)
        #expect(export.evaluatedNodes.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func progressReachesTheCaller() async throws {
        let probe = makeNode(QualityProbeNode.self, output: true)
        let seen = Mutex<[Double]>([])
        _ = try await evaluator().evaluate(graph([probe]), demand: [probe.id], progress: MeshProgress { fraction in
            seen.withLock { $0.append(fraction) }
        })
        #expect(seen.withLock { $0 } == [0.25, 0.75])
    }

    @Test func progressIsHeldBetweenZeroAndOne() {
        let seen = Mutex<[Double]>([])
        let progress = MeshProgress { fraction in seen.withLock { $0.append(fraction) } }
        progress(-1)
        progress(2)
        #expect(seen.withLock { $0 } == [0, 1])
    }

    @MainActor
    @Test func aDocumentTakesTheEngine() async {
        let document = DocumentModel(registry: meshProbeRegistry, kernel: FakeKernel(), implicitEngine: .automatic)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(document.graph.nodes.isEmpty)
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/MeshContextProbeNodes.swift`**

````swift
// Test fixture file: it deliberately holds two small node definitions.
import CreatorKernel
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// Reports the mesh quality and engine its context carries, and some progress; reads the quality, so its cache key holds it.
enum QualityProbeNode: NodeDefinition {
    static let typeID = "test.qualityProbe"
    static let displayName = "Quality Probe"
    static let category = NodeCategory.value
    static let readsMeshQuality = true
    static let inputs: [SocketSpec] = []
    static let outputs = [SocketSpec("quality", .integer), SocketSpec("engine", .integer)]

    static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        context.meshProgress(0.25)
        context.meshProgress(0.75)
        return NodeOutputs([
            "quality": .integer(context.meshQuality == .export ? 1 : 0),
            "engine": .integer(context.implicitEngine == .automatic ? 1 : 0),
        ])
    }
}

/// The same, without reading the quality: a result it makes is shared by previews and exports.
enum PlainProbeNode: NodeDefinition {
    static let typeID = "test.plainProbe"
    static let displayName = "Plain Probe"
    static let category = NodeCategory.value
    static let inputs: [SocketSpec] = []
    static let outputs = [SocketSpec("quality", .integer)]

    static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        NodeOutputs(["quality": .integer(context.meshQuality == .export ? 1 : 0)])
    }
}

let meshProbeRegistry = NodeRegistry([QualityProbeNode.self, PlainProbeNode.self])
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// Engine selection, tested with `StubGPUEngine` so it runs on every platform; the Metal engine's own cases follow, where Metal exists.
struct ImplicitEvaluatorsTests {
    static let profileSolid: ImplicitSolid = {
        let profile = Profile2D.rectangle(width: 4, height: 4, plane: .xy)
        let graded = Grading(field: .distance(.profile(profile), signed: false), range: 0...10, slope: 1)
        return ImplicitSolid.offset(.sphere(radius: 3), distance: graded)
    }()

    @Test func theCPUChoiceIsAlwaysTheCPUWithNoWarning() async {
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: ImplicitFixtures.sphere, choice: .cpu, engine: StubGPUEngine())
        #expect(selection.evaluator.engineName == "CPU" && selection.warning == nil)
    }

    @Test func noGPUEngineMeansTheCPUAndNoWarning() async {
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: ImplicitFixtures.sphere, choice: .automatic, engine: nil)
        #expect(selection.evaluator.engineName == "CPU" && selection.warning == nil && selection.diagnostic == nil)
    }

    @Test func automaticUsesTheGPUEngineWhenItCan() async throws {
        let engine = StubGPUEngine()
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: ImplicitFixtures.cylinder, choice: .automatic, engine: engine)
        #expect(selection.evaluator.engineName == "Stub" && selection.warning == nil)
        let values = try await selection.evaluator.evaluate([SIMD3(0, 0, 100)])
        #expect(values.count == 1 && values[0] > 0)
        #expect(await engine.prepared == 1)
    }

    @Test func aSolidTheGPUCannotDoRunsOnTheCPUWithAWarning() async {
        let engine = StubGPUEngine(failure: .unsupported("a distance to a profile"))
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: Self.profileSolid, choice: .automatic, engine: engine)
        #expect(selection.evaluator.engineName == "CPU" && selection.warning == "Running on the CPU")
        #expect(selection.diagnostic?.contains("a distance to a profile") == true)
    }

    @Test func aCompilerFailureAlsoFallsBackToTheCPUWithAWarning() async {
        let engine = StubGPUEngine(failure: .engine("a made-up error"))
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: ImplicitFixtures.sphere, choice: .automatic, engine: engine)
        #expect(selection.evaluator.engineName == "CPU" && selection.warning == ImplicitEvaluators.cpuWarning)
    }

    @Test func aMeshFromTheCPUEngineIsClosedAndManifold() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitFixtures.all[5].solid
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: solid, choice: .cpu)
        let mesh = try await ImplicitMesher(evaluator: selection.evaluator).mesh(solid, resolution: 0.5, quality: .preview)
        #expect(MeshTopology(mesh).isClosedManifold)
    }
}
````

````swift
/// Which engine evaluates implicit solids (implicit spec §4.1). The evaluator carries it to the Mesh node in `EvalContext`.
public enum ImplicitEngineChoice: String, Sendable, Hashable {
    /// The CPU engine, always. A context made by hand (the tests) has this.
    case cpu
    /// The GPU engine when the machine has one and the solid compiles, else the CPU (the CPU is a complete engine: Errata D11).
    case automatic
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorGraph/Implicit/MeshProgress.swift`**

````swift
/// Where a long meshing reports how far it has got (implicit spec §5.3): a fraction from 0 to 1, called from any thread. The export
/// sheet's progress bar listens; a run made by hand ignores it.
public struct MeshProgress: Sendable {
    private let report: @Sendable (Double) -> Void

    public init(_ report: @escaping @Sendable (Double) -> Void) {
        self.report = report
    }

    /// Progress that goes nowhere.
    public static let none = MeshProgress { _ in }

    public func callAsFunction(_ fraction: Double) {
        report(min(max(fraction, 0), 1))
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/GPU/ImplicitEvaluators.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph

/// Picks the engine that evaluates a solid (implicit spec §4.1): a GPU engine when asked for and available, else the CPU, which is a
/// complete engine on every platform (Errata D11). Names no GPU API: the engine comes from `ImplicitGPUEngines`.
public enum ImplicitEvaluators {
    /// The engine chosen, and whether it is a fall-back the person should hear about.
    public struct Selection: Sendable {
        public var evaluator: any ImplicitBatchEvaluator
        /// "Running on the CPU" when a GPU engine was wanted and the solid would not compile; `nil` otherwise, including on a machine
        /// with no GPU engine, where the CPU is all there is.
        public var warning: String?
        /// Why the GPU engine was not used, for the log, when it was wanted and the solid would not compile. Never shown to the person.
        public var diagnostic: String?
    }

    /// The warning's text.
    public static let cpuWarning = "Running on the CPU"

    /// The evaluator for `solid`. Compiling runs here, off the main actor when the caller is. `engine` is the machine's by default; the
    /// tests pass their own.
    public static func make(for solid: ImplicitSolid, choice: ImplicitEngineChoice,
                            engine: (any ImplicitGPUEngine)? = ImplicitGPUEngines.platformDefault) async -> Selection {
        let cpu = CPUBatchEvaluator(solid)
        guard choice == .automatic, let engine else { return Selection(evaluator: cpu, warning: nil, diagnostic: nil) }
        do {
            let program = try await engine.compile(solid)
            return Selection(evaluator: GPUBatchEvaluator(engine: engine, program: program), warning: nil, diagnostic: nil)
        } catch ImplicitCompileError.unsupported(let what) {
            // Not a bug: some parts have no GPU form yet. The CPU does it, and the person is told.
            return Selection(evaluator: cpu, warning: cpuWarning, diagnostic: "no GPU form: \(what)")
        } catch {
            return Selection(evaluator: cpu, warning: cpuWarning, diagnostic: "the GPU compiler failed: \(error)")
        }
    }
}
````

````diff
+    case engine(String)
````

**Notes for the implementer:**

- The node names carry `Implicit` to keep them apart from the B-rep Boolean's Union / Subtract / Intersect operations; the palette shows the spec's display names (Sphere, Box, Cylinder, Union, Subtract, Intersect).
- A list of implicits into `shapes` is one combination (the socket takes the whole list); a list into a number socket (`radius`) broadcasts into a list of solids.
- `CatalogTests` already checks every built-in node's inspector controls against its sockets, so the new nodes are covered by it.

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorNodes
import Testing
@testable import CreatorEditor

/// The node library's "Implicit" category (implicit spec §3): after Fields, before Output.
struct ImplicitLibraryTests {
    var sections: [LibrarySection] {
        LibrarySection.grouping(BuiltInNodes.all.map {
            PaletteEntry(typeID: $0.typeID, displayName: $0.displayName, category: $0.category)
        })
    }

    @Test func theImplicitSectionFollowsFieldsAndPrecedesOutput() {
        let titles = sections.map(\.title)
        #expect(titles.suffix(3) == ["Fields", "Implicit", "Output"])
    }

    @Test func theImplicitSectionHoldsTheImplicitNodesInOrder() {
        let names = sections.first { $0.category == .implicit }?.entries.map(\.displayName)
        #expect(names == ["Sphere", "Box", "Cylinder", "Union", "Subtract", "Intersect"])
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/ImplicitCombineNodeTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Union, Subtract and Intersect (implicit spec §3.3).
struct ImplicitCombineNodeTests {
    /// Spheres of radius 10 and 5 as one list.
    func spheres(_ h: inout Harness) -> Node {
        let radii = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["start": .number(10), "step": .number(-5), "count": .integer(2)])
        let spheres = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        h.wire(radii, "values", to: spheres, "radius")
        return spheres
    }

    @Test func aUnionOfTwoShapesIsTheirUnion() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        let report = try await h.run([union])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(union))
        #expect(solid.description == "union(sphere(radius 10 mm), sphere(radius 5 mm))")
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == -10 && solid.distance(12, 0, 0) == 2)
        #expect(report.isOK(union))
    }

    @Test func subtractCutsTheLaterShapesFromTheFirst() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let subtract = h.add(ImplicitSubtractNode.self)
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: subtract, "shapes")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([subtract]).implicitSolid(subtract))
        #expect(solid.description == "subtract(sphere(radius 10 mm), sphere(radius 5 mm))")
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == 5, "the small ball is cut from the middle")
        #expect(solid.distance(8, 0, 0) == -2)
    }

    @Test func intersectKeepsWhatIsInEveryShape() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let intersect = h.add(ImplicitIntersectNode.self)
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: intersect, "shapes")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([intersect]).implicitSolid(intersect))
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == -5 && solid.distance(8, 0, 0) == 3)
    }

    @Test func aBlendRadiusRoundsTheJoin() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self, ["blend radius": .number(2)])
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([union]).implicitSolid(union))
        #expect(solid.description == "union(sphere(radius 10 mm), sphere(radius 5 mm), blend 2 mm)")
        // Both spheres read −5 at 5 mm from the centre along... the smaller one's surface: it is inside the bigger by 5, so no dip.
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == -10)
        #expect(solid.bounds.max.x == 10.5, "a smooth union may bulge a quarter of the blend radius")
    }

    @Test func oneSharpShapeIsPassedThrough() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(4)])
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([union]).implicitSolid(union))
        #expect(solid.description == "sphere(radius 4 mm)")
    }

    @Test func noShapesWaitForOne() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([union])
        guard case .idle(let reason?)? = report.state(union) else { Issue.record("expected idle"); return }
        #expect(reason == "Connect or set “shapes”.")
    }

    @Test func anEmptyListOfShapesIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let none = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(0)])
        let spheres = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        h.wire(none, "values", to: spheres, "radius")
        h.wire(spheres, "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        let report = try await h.run([union])
        #expect(report.error(union) == "Connect at least one shape to “shapes”.")
    }

    @Test func aFieldGradesTheBlendAndNegativeValuesAreWarnedAbout() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(10)])
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        h.wire(gradient, "field", to: union, "blend radius")
        let report = try await h.run([union])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(union))
        #expect(solid.description.contains("blend linear(length 10)"))
        #expect(solid.lipschitz > 1, "a graded blend raises the lipschitz bound")
        #expect(report.warning(union) == "“blend radius” is below 0 in places; it is held at 0 there.")
    }

    @Test func aDistanceToASolidGradesTheBlendThroughABake() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        let clamp = h.add(FieldClampNode.self, ["low": .number(0), "high": .number(3)])
        let union = h.add(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        h.wire(distance, "field", to: clamp, "field")
        h.wire(clamp, "field", to: union, "blend radius")
        h.wire(spheres(&h), "implicit", to: union, "shapes")
        let report = try await h.run([union], kernel: kernel)
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(union), "\(String(describing: report.state(union)))")
        guard case .union(_, let blend) = solid else { Issue.record("not a union"); return }
        #expect(blend.baked.count == 1 && blend.range == 0...3)
        #expect(report.isOK(union))
    }
}
````

````swift
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Sphere, Box and Cylinder (implicit spec §3.2).
struct ImplicitPrimitiveNodeTests {
    @Test func theDefaultSphereIsTenMillimetresOfRadius() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([sphere])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(sphere))
        #expect(solid.description == "sphere(radius 10 mm)")
        #expect(solid.distance(10, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == -10)
        #expect(solid.lipschitz == 1)
    }

    @Test func aSphereRefusesAnEmptyRadius() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let zero = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(0)])
        let infinite = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(.infinity)])
        let report = try await h.run([zero, infinite])
        #expect(report.error(zero) == "“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(infinite) == "“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.")
    }

    @Test func aBoxIsCentredAndRoundedOnRequest() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(20, 10, 4)), "corner radius": .number(1)])
        let report = try await h.run([box])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(box))
        #expect(solid.description == "box(20 × 10 × 4 mm, corner radius 1 mm)")
        #expect(solid.distance(10, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(0, 5, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(0, 0, 2) == 0)
        #expect(solid.bounds == BoundingBox(min: Vector3(-10, -5, -2), max: Vector3(10, 5, 2)))
    }

    @Test func theDefaultBoxIsASharpTwentyMillimetreCube() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self)
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([box]).implicitSolid(box))
        #expect(solid.description == "box(20 × 20 × 20 mm)")
    }

    @Test func aBoxRefusesAnEmptySideOrACornerThatDoesNotFit() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let flat = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(10, 0, 10))])
        let round = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(10, 10, 4)), "corner radius": .number(2.5)])
        let negative = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["corner radius": .number(-1)])
        let report = try await h.run([flat, round, negative])
        #expect(report.error(flat) == "“size” must be greater than 0 mm on every side.")
        #expect(report.error(round) == "“corner radius” can't be more than half the smallest side.")
        #expect(report.error(negative) == "“corner radius” must be 0 or more.")
    }

    @Test func aCylinderStandsOnItsAxisAlongZ() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let cylinder = h.add(ImplicitCylinderNode.self, ["radius": .number(3), "height": .number(8)])
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([cylinder]).implicitSolid(cylinder))
        #expect(solid.description == "cylinder(radius 3 mm, height 8 mm)")
        #expect(solid.distance(3, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(0, 0, 4) == 0 && solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == -3)
    }

    @Test func aCylinderRefusesAnEmptyRadiusOrHeight() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let thin = h.add(ImplicitCylinderNode.self, ["radius": .number(0)])
        let flat = h.add(ImplicitCylinderNode.self, ["height": .number(-1)])
        let report = try await h.run([thin, flat])
        #expect(report.error(thin) == "“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(flat) == "“height” must be greater than 0 mm.")
    }

    @Test func aListOfRadiiMakesAListOfSpheres() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let radii = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["start": .number(2), "step": .number(3), "count": .integer(3)])
        let spheres = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        h.wire(radii, "values", to: spheres, "radius")
        let solids = try #require(try await h.run([spheres]).value(spheres, "implicit")?.implicitSolids)
        #expect(solids.map(\.description) == ["sphere(radius 2 mm)", "sphere(radius 5 mm)", "sphere(radius 8 mm)"])
    }

    @Test func theNodesLiveInTheImplicitCategory() {
        for node: any NodeDefinition.Type in [ImplicitSphereNode.self, ImplicitBoxNode.self, ImplicitCylinderNode.self] {
            #expect(node.category == .implicit && node.outputs.map(\.type) == [.implicit])
        }
        let names = [ImplicitSphereNode.displayName, ImplicitBoxNode.displayName, ImplicitCylinderNode.displayName]
        #expect(names == ["Sphere", "Box", "Cylinder"])
    }
}
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Testing

extension Value {
    /// The implicit solids carried, or `nil` if any item isn't one.
    var implicitSolids: [ImplicitSolid]? {
        typed { if case .implicit(let reference) = $0 { reference.solid } else { nil } }
    }
}

extension EvaluationReport {
    /// The one implicit solid on `socket` of `node`.
    func implicitSolid(_ node: Node, _ socket: SocketName = "implicit") -> ImplicitSolid? {
        guard let solids = value(node, socket)?.implicitSolids, solids.count == 1 else { return nil }
        return solids[0]
    }
}

extension ImplicitSolid {
    func distance(_ x: Double, _ y: Double, _ z: Double) -> Double { signedDistance(at: Vector3(x, y, z)) }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitBoxNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// A box about the origin as an implicit solid, with rounded edges if asked (implicit spec §3.2).
public enum ImplicitBoxNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.implicitBox"
    public static let displayName = "Box"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("size", .vector, defaultValue: .vector(Vector3(20, 20, 20)), unit: .millimetres),
        SocketSpec("corner radius", .number, defaultValue: .number(0), unit: .millimetres, range: 0...50),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Box", controls: [.vector("size"), .slider("corner radius")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let size = try inputs.vector("size")
        let radius = try inputs.number("corner radius")
        guard size.isFinite, size.x > 0, size.y > 0, size.z > 0 else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“size” must be greater than 0 mm on every side.")
        }
        guard radius.isFinite, radius >= 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“corner radius” must be 0 or more.") }
        guard radius <= min(size.x, size.y, size.z) / 2 else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“corner radius” can't be more than half the smallest side.")
        }
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: size * 0.5, cornerRadius: radius).reference)])
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitCombining.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// What Union, Subtract and Intersect share (implicit spec §3.3): a list of shapes, a blend radius (a field, so a 7b field can grade
/// it; 0 is sharp), and the result.
enum ImplicitCombining {
    enum Kind {
        case union, subtract, intersect

        /// The solid the shapes make with `blend`.
        func combine(_ shapes: [ImplicitSolid], blend: Grading) -> ImplicitSolid {
            switch self {
            case .union: .union(shapes, blend: blend)
            case .subtract: .subtract(shapes, blend: blend)
            case .intersect: .intersect(shapes, blend: blend)
            }
        }
    }

    static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("shapes", .implicit, access: .list),
        SocketSpec("blend radius", .field, defaultValue: .number(0), unit: .millimetres, range: 0...50),
    ]
    static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]

    static func inspector(_ title: String) -> [InspectorSection] {
        [InspectorSection(title: title, controls: [.number("blend radius")])]
    }

    static func evaluate(_ kind: Kind, _ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let shapes = try inputs.implicitSolids("shapes")
        guard !shapes.isEmpty else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Connect at least one shape to “shapes”.") }
        let region = ImplicitGrading.region(around: kind.combine(shapes, blend: .constant(0)).bounds)
        let setting = try await ImplicitGrading.make(try inputs.field("blend radius"), named: "blend radius", over: region,
                                                     holdsAtZero: true, kernel: kernel, context: context)
        // One shape, sharp, is that shape; anything else is the combination.
        let sharp = setting.grading.constantValue.map { $0 <= 0 } ?? false
        let result = shapes.count == 1 && sharp ? shapes[0] : kind.combine(shapes, blend: setting.grading)
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(result.reference)], warnings: setting.warnings)
    }
}
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// A cylinder about the origin as an implicit solid, its axis along Z (implicit spec §3.2).
public enum ImplicitCylinderNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.implicitCylinder"
    public static let displayName = "Cylinder"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("radius", .number, defaultValue: .number(5), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
        SocketSpec("height", .number, defaultValue: .number(20), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Cylinder", controls: [.slider("radius"), .slider("height")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let (radius, height) = (try inputs.number("radius"), try inputs.number("height"))
        guard radius.isFinite, radius > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard height.isFinite, height > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“height” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(ImplicitSolid.cylinder(radius: radius, halfHeight: height / 2).reference)])
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitGrading.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// Turns the field wired into a setting (a blend radius, a thickness, an offset) into the `Grading` an implicit solid holds, and says
/// what is worth warning about (implicit spec §3.3).
enum ImplicitGrading {
    /// What a setting came to: the grading, and the warnings it earned.
    struct Result {
        var grading: Grading
        var warnings: [String]
    }

    /// The grading of `name`'s field over `region`.
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - holdsAtZero: whether a value below 0 is held at 0 (a blend radius, a thickness), which earns a warning where the field is.
    static func make(_ field: Field, named name: SocketName, over region: BoundingBox, holdsAtZero: Bool,
                     kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> Result {
        let grading = try await GradingBaker.grading(for: field, over: region, kernel: kernel, cache: context.fieldDistances)
        var warnings: [String] = []
        if !field.slopeBound.isKnown {
            warnings.append("“\(name)” changes at a rate that isn't known, so the preview may be slow.")
        }
        if holdsAtZero, grading.constantValue == nil, grading.range.lowerBound < 0 {
            warnings.append("“\(name)” is below 0 in places; it is held at 0 there.")
        }
        return Result(grading: grading, warnings: warnings)
    }

    /// A box around `box` with room for a setting that makes the shape bigger: 20% of its longest side, at least a millimetre.
    static func region(around box: BoundingBox) -> BoundingBox {
        let longest = max(box.size.x, box.size.y, box.size.z)
        return ImplicitSolid.grown(box, by: max(1, longest * 0.2))
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/NodeInputs+Implicits.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit

extension NodeInputs {
    /// The implicit solid on `name` (an item socket).
    func implicitSolid(_ name: SocketName) throws -> ImplicitSolid {
        try solid(of: try implicit(name), name)
    }

    /// Every implicit solid on the list socket `name`, in order.
    func implicitSolids(_ name: SocketName) throws -> [ImplicitSolid] {
        try list(name).map { scalar in
            guard case .implicit(let reference) = scalar else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch(name, expected: .implicit) }
            return try solid(of: reference, name)
        }
    }

    private func solid(of reference: ImplicitReference, _ name: SocketName) throws -> ImplicitSolid {
        guard let solid = reference.solid else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch(name, expected: .implicit) }
        return solid
    }
}
````

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Shell and Offset (implicit spec §3.3).
struct ImplicitShellOffsetNodeTests {
    func sphere(_ h: inout Harness, radius: Double = 10) -> Node {
        h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(radius)])
    }

    @Test func aShellIsAnInwardWallByDefault() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self)
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        let report = try await h.run([shell])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(shell))
        #expect(solid.description == "shell(sphere(radius 10 mm), inward 2 mm)")
        #expect(solid.distance(10, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(8, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(9, 0, 0) == -1)
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) == 8)
        #expect(report.isOK(shell))
    }

    @Test(arguments: [(1, "outward", 12.0, 10.0), (2, "centred", 11.0, 9.0)])
    func theSideChoosesWhereTheWallLies(_ side: Int, _ name: String, _ outer: Double, _ inner: Double) async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self, ["side": .integer(side)])
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([shell]).implicitSolid(shell))
        #expect(solid.description == "shell(sphere(radius 10 mm), \(name) 2 mm)")
        #expect(solid.distance(outer, 0, 0) == 0 && solid.distance(inner, 0, 0) == 0)
    }

    @Test func anUnknownSideIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self, ["side": .integer(7)])
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        #expect(try await h.run([shell]).error(shell) == "Choose Inward, Outward, or Centred for “side”.")
    }

    @Test func aFieldGradesTheThickness() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(20)])
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self)
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        h.wire(gradient, "field", to: shell, "thickness")
        let report = try await h.run([shell])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(shell))
        // z / 20 is 0 on the XY plane and 0.5 at z = 10: no wall in the lower half, 0.5 mm at the top.
        #expect(solid.lipschitz == 1.05)
        // At z = 9.75 the thickness is 9.75 / 20 and the sphere's surface is 0.25 mm above.
        let thickness = 9.75 / 20
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(0, 0, 9.75) - (abs(-0.25 + thickness / 2) - thickness / 2)) < 1e-12)
        #expect(solid.distance(0, 0, -5) == 5, "no wall where the thickness is 0 or less: the value is the distance to the surface")
        #expect(report.warning(shell) == "“thickness” is below 0 in places; it is held at 0 there.")
    }

    @Test func aThicknessWithoutAKnownSlopeWarnsThatThePreviewMayBeSlow() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        let product = h.add(FieldMultiplyNode.self)
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self)
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        h.wire(distance, "field", to: product, "a")
        h.wire(distance, "field", to: product, "b")
        h.wire(product, "field", to: shell, "thickness")
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        let report = try await h.run([shell], kernel: kernel)
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(shell))
        #expect(report.warning(shell)?.contains("“thickness” changes at a rate that isn't known, so the preview may be slow.") == true)
        #expect(solid.lipschitz >= 1 + Field.unknownSlope)
    }

    @Test func anOffsetMovesTheSurface() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let grow = h.add(ImplicitOffsetNode.self)
        let shrink = h.add(ImplicitOffsetNode.self, ["distance": .number(-3)])
        let ball = sphere(&h)
        h.wire(ball, "implicit", to: grow, "implicit")
        h.wire(ball, "implicit", to: shrink, "implicit")
        let report = try await h.run([grow, shrink])
        let (big, small) = (try #require(report.implicitSolid(grow)), try #require(report.implicitSolid(shrink)))
        #expect(big.description == "offset(sphere(radius 10 mm), 1 mm)" && big.distance(11, 0, 0) == 0)
        #expect(small.description == "offset(sphere(radius 10 mm), −3 mm)" && small.distance(7, 0, 0) == 0)
        #expect(report.isOK(grow) && report.isOK(shrink), "a negative offset is no warning")
    }

    @Test func wavesInAnOffsetFollowTheField() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let noise = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["scale": .number(5)])
        let offset = h.add(ImplicitOffsetNode.self)
        h.wire(sphere(&h), "implicit", to: offset, "implicit")
        h.wire(noise, "field", to: offset, "distance")
        let report = try await h.run([offset])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(offset))
        #expect(report.isOK(offset))
        // The noise is in 0…1, so the surface moved out by up to a millimetre.
        #expect(solid.bounds.max.x == 11 && solid.lipschitz > 1)
    }

    @Test func aShellAndAnOffsetNeedAnImplicit() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self)
        let offset = h.add(ImplicitOffsetNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([shell, offset])
        guard case .idle(let reason?)? = report.state(shell) else { Issue.record("expected idle"); return }
        #expect(reason == "Connect or set “implicit”.")
        guard case .idle? = report.state(offset) else { Issue.record("expected idle"); return }
    }

    @Test func aWallThickerThanABakedSolidsBandWarns() {
        let size = DistanceBlocks.blockSize
        let blocks = DistanceBlocks(origin: .zero, spacing: 1, blockCounts: [1, 1, 1], table: [0],
                                    pool: [Float](repeating: 1, count: size * size * size), coarse: [0], band: 3)
        let baked = ImplicitSolid.baked(blocks, label: "Part")
        #expect(ImplicitReach.warnings(for: baked, reachedBy: 2).isEmpty)
        #expect(ImplicitReach.warnings(for: baked, reachedBy: 5)
            == ["The distance reaches 5 mm from the surface, past the 3 mm To Implicit measured exactly, so it may be inaccurate."])
        #expect(ImplicitReach.warnings(for: .sphere(radius: 3), reachedBy: 500).isEmpty, "an analytic solid is exact everywhere")
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitOffsetNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// Moves an implicit solid's surface by `distance`, outwards when positive and inwards when negative (implicit spec §3.3). The
/// distance is a field, so a 7b field can grade it.
public enum ImplicitOffsetNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.implicitOffset"
    public static let displayName = "Offset"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit),
        SocketSpec("distance", .field, defaultValue: .number(1), unit: .millimetres, range: -50...50),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Offset", controls: [.number("distance")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let child = try inputs.implicitSolid("implicit")
        let setting = try await ImplicitGrading.make(try inputs.field("distance"), named: "distance",
                                                     over: ImplicitGrading.region(around: child.bounds), holdsAtZero: false,
                                                     kernel: kernel, context: context)
        let result = ImplicitSolid.offset(child, distance: setting.grading)
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(result.reference)],
                           warnings: setting.warnings + ImplicitReach.warnings(for: child, reachedBy: setting.grading.magnitude))
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitReach.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// A baked solid's distance is exact only within its band (implicit spec §4.4); a shell or an offset that reaches past it is wrong
/// beyond it, so the node says so.
enum ImplicitReach {
    static func warnings(for solid: ImplicitSolid, reachedBy distance: Double) -> [String] {
        let reach = solid.exactReach
        guard reach.isFinite, distance > reach else { return [] }
        let (asked, measured) = (distance.millimetreText(locale: .messages), reach.millimetreText(locale: .messages))
        let text = "The distance reaches \(asked) mm from the surface, past the \(measured) mm To Implicit measured exactly, "
            + "so it may be inaccurate."
        return [text]
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ImplicitShellNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// A wall of `thickness` on one side of an implicit solid's surface (implicit spec §3.3): inside it, outside it or centred on it.
/// The thickness is a field, so a 7b field can grade it; it is sampled per point by the solid's own evaluator.
public enum ImplicitShellNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.implicitShell"
    public static let displayName = "Shell"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit),
        SocketSpec("thickness", .field, defaultValue: .number(2), unit: .millimetres, range: 0...50),
        SocketSpec("side", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(ShellSide.inward.rawValue)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Shell", controls: [.segmented("side", options: ShellSide.options), .number("thickness")]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let child = try inputs.implicitSolid("implicit")
        // `choice` refuses an index outside the options, so every index it returns is a side.
        let side = ShellSide(rawValue: try inputs.choice("side", options: ShellSide.options)) ?? .inward
        let setting = try await ImplicitGrading.make(try inputs.field("thickness"), named: "thickness",
                                                     over: ImplicitGrading.region(around: child.bounds), holdsAtZero: true,
                                                     kernel: kernel, context: context)
        let result = ImplicitSolid.shell(child, thickness: setting.grading, side: side)
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(result.reference)],
                           warnings: setting.warnings + ImplicitReach.warnings(for: child, reachedBy: setting.grading.magnitude))
    }
}
````

**Notes for the implementer:**

- To Implicit's label in the recipe is the node's name when the person renamed it ("implicit(Bracket)"), else "solid".
- Isosurface takes its region from the field's support when it has one (only an implicit read as a field does) and otherwise from the `bounds` input: a solid, an implicit, or a list of corner points. A max slope smaller than the field's declared slope warns that the preview may have holes.
- The OCCT-backed test builds a filleted 20 × 20 × 10 box and compares the bake with the exact rounded box: within 0.2 mm within 1.5 mm of the surface at the test's coarse 0.04 mm tolerance (spacing 0.8 mm across a 2 mm fillet), and within 0.3 mm deeper, where trilinear interpolation cuts the medial ridge short.
