````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorNodes
import Testing
@testable import CreatorEditor

/// Implicit solids in the editor (implicit spec §2.1, §5.2): the inspector's "Implicit" row and the socket tooltip.
@MainActor
struct ImplicitDisplayTests {
    let registry = BuiltInNodes.registry

    func node(_ definition: any NodeDefinition.Type, _ values: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) -> Node {
        var made = registry.makeNode(definition.typeID)
        made.inputValues.merge(values) { _, given in given }
        made.isOutput = true
        return made
    }

    func editor(_ nodes: [Node], _ links: [Link] = []) async -> EditorModel {
        let editor = makeEditor(nodes, links, registry: registry)
        await editor.document.waitForEvaluation()
        return editor
    }

    func section(_ editor: EditorModel, _ node: Node, _ title: String) -> InspectorSectionRows? {
        editor.selection = [node.id]
        return editor.inspectorPage.sections.first { $0.title == title }
    }

    @Test func aNodeWithAnImplicitOutputShowsItsRecipe() async {
        let sphere = node(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(7.5)])
        let editor = await editor([sphere])
        #expect(section(editor, sphere, "Implicit") == InspectorSectionRows(title: "Implicit", rows: [
            .recipe(label: "Implicit", text: "sphere(radius 7.5 mm)"),
        ]))
    }

    @Test func theRecipeFollowsTheWiring() async {
        let sphere = node(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let shell = node(ImplicitShellNode.self)
        let editor = await editor([sphere, shell], [wire(sphere, "implicit", shell, "implicit")])
        #expect(section(editor, shell, "Implicit")?.rows == [.recipe(label: "Implicit", text: "shell(sphere(radius 10 mm), inward 2 mm)")])
    }

    @Test func beforeAResultTheRowSaysSo() {
        let sphere = node(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let editor = makeEditor([sphere], [], registry: registry)
        #expect(section(editor, sphere, "Implicit")?.rows == [.recipe(label: "Implicit", text: "No implicit solid yet")])
    }

    @Test func aNodeThatMakesNoImplicitHasNoImplicitRow() async {
        let number = node(NumberNode.self)
        let editor = await editor([number])
        #expect(section(editor, number, "Implicit") == nil && section(editor, number, "Mesh") == nil)
    }

    @Test func theTooltipShowsTheRecipeOnTheOutputAndOnTheInputItIsWiredInto() async {
        let sphere = node(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let union = node(ImplicitUnionNode.self)
        let editor = await editor([sphere, union], [wire(sphere, "implicit", union, "shapes")])
        #expect(editor.socketHelp(of: sphere.id)["out.implicit"] == "sphere(radius 10 mm)")
        #expect(editor.socketHelp(of: union.id)["in.shapes"] == "sphere(radius 10 mm)")
    }

    @Test func aLongRecipeIsCutToEightyCharacters() async {
        let sphere = node(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        var chain = [sphere]
        var links: [Link] = []
        for step in 0..<4 {
            let next = node(step.isMultiple(of: 2) ? ImplicitShellNode.self : ImplicitOffsetNode.self)
            links.append(wire(chain[chain.count - 1], "implicit", next, "implicit"))
            chain.append(next)
        }
        let editor = await editor(chain, links)
        let tip = editor.socketHelp(of: chain[chain.count - 1].id)["out.implicit"]
        #expect(tip?.count == 80 && tip?.hasSuffix("…") == true)
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/IsosurfaceNodeTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Isosurface (implicit spec §3.1).
struct IsosurfaceNodeTests {
    static let help = "Wire a solid, an implicit solid or two corner points into “bounds” to say where to fill."

    /// A radial gradient of radius 20 about the origin: |p| / 20.
    func radial(_ h: inout Harness, radius: Double = 20) -> Node { h.add(RadialGradientNode.self, ["radius": .number(radius)]) }

    /// An isosurface of `radial` at level 0.5 with max slope 0.05, which is a ball of radius 10.
    func isosurface(_ h: inout Harness, radius: Double = 20, slope: Double = 0.05) -> Node {
        let iso = h.add(IsosurfaceNode.self, ["level": .number(0.5), "max slope": .number(slope)])
        h.wire(radial(&h, radius: radius), "field", to: iso, "field")
        return iso
    }

    /// Two vectors, (low, low, low) and (high, high, high), as one list.
    func corners(_ h: inout Harness, _ low: Double, _ high: Double) -> Node {
        let series = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["start": .number(low), "step": .number(high - low), "count": .integer(2)])
        let vectors = h.add(VectorNode.self)
        for axis in ["x", "y", "z"] { h.wire(series, "values", to: vectors, SocketName(axis)) }
        return vectors
    }

    @Test func aFieldBelowALevelIsASolid() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let cube = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(40, 40, 40))])
        let iso = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(cube, "implicit", to: iso, "bounds")
        let report = try await h.run([iso])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(iso))
        // |p| / 20 < 0.5 is a ball of radius 10, and (|p| / 20 − 0.5) ÷ 0.05 is |p| − 10.
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(0, 0, 0) + 10) < 1e-9 && abs(solid.distance(10, 0, 0)) < 1e-9)
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(14, 0, 0) - 4) < 1e-9)
        #expect(solid.lipschitz == 1)
        #expect(solid.description == "isosurface(radial(radius 20), level 0.5, max slope 0.05)")
        #expect(report.isOK(iso))
    }

    @Test func twoCornersNameTheRegion() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let iso = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(corners(&h, -15, 15), "vector", to: iso, "bounds")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([iso]).implicitSolid(iso))
        #expect(solid.bounds == BoundingBox(min: Vector3(-15, -15, -15), max: Vector3(15, 15, 15)))
    }

    @Test func theRegionClipsTheSolid() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let iso = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(corners(&h, 0, 12), "vector", to: iso, "bounds")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([iso]).implicitSolid(iso))
        #expect(solid.distance(5, 5, 5) < 0, "inside the ball and the region")
        #expect(solid.distance(-5, 5, 5) == 5, "inside the ball but 5 mm outside the region: the region's distance wins")
        #expect(solid.distance(14, 14, 14) > 0)
    }

    @Test func aSolidsBoxNamesTheRegionToo() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let iso = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(h.box(30, 30, 30), "solid", to: iso, "bounds")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([iso]).implicitSolid(iso))
        #expect(solid.bounds == BoundingBox(min: Vector3(-15, -15, 0), max: Vector3(15, 15, 30)))
    }

    @Test func anImplicitSolidReadAsAFieldNeedsNoBounds() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let ball = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let iso = h.add(IsosurfaceNode.self, ["level": .number(3)])
        h.wire(ball, "implicit", to: iso, "field")
        let report = try await h.run([iso])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(iso), "\(String(describing: report.state(iso)))")
        // Inside where the distance to the ball is below 3: a ball of radius 13, in a region 3 mm wider than the ball's box.
        #expect(solid.description == "isosurface(implicit(sphere(radius 10 mm)), level 3, max slope 1)")
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(13, 0, 0)) < 1e-9 && solid.bounds.max.x == 13)
        #expect(report.isOK(iso))
    }

    @Test func aDistanceToASolidIsBakedOverTheRegion() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let part = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        let iso = h.add(IsosurfaceNode.self, ["level": .number(2)])
        h.wire(part, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        h.wire(distance, "field", to: iso, "field")
        h.wire(h.box(30, 30, 30), "solid", to: iso, "bounds")
        let report = try await h.run([iso])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(iso), "\(String(describing: report.state(iso)))")
        guard case .isosurface(let grading, _, _, _) = solid else { Issue.record("not an isosurface"); return }
        #expect(grading.baked.count == 1)
        // The signed distance below 2 is the part grown by 2 mm: 7 mm out from the middle along x, the grid being a little coarse.
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(7, 0, 5)) < 0.2)
    }

    @Test func aRegionIsRequiredAndMustHaveVolume() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let missing = isosurface(&h)
        let flat = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(corners(&h, 3, 3), "vector", to: flat, "bounds")
        let wrong = isosurface(&h)
        h.wire(h.add(NumberNode.self), "value", to: wrong, "bounds")
        let report = try await h.run([missing, flat, wrong])
        #expect(report.error(missing) == "Isosurface needs a region to fill. \(Self.help)")
        #expect(report.error(flat) == "The region to fill has no volume. \(Self.help)")
        #expect(report.error(wrong) == "“bounds” takes a solid, an implicit solid or two points. \(Self.help)")
    }

    @Test func aMaxSlopeOfZeroOrLessIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let iso = isosurface(&h, slope: 0)
        h.wire(corners(&h, -15, 15), "vector", to: iso, "bounds")
        #expect(try await h.run([iso]).error(iso) == "“max slope” must be greater than 0.")
    }

    @Test func aMaxSlopeSmallerThanTheFieldChangesWarnsOfHoles() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let iso = isosurface(&h, radius: 5, slope: 0.05)
        h.wire(corners(&h, -15, 15), "vector", to: iso, "bounds")
        let report = try await h.run([iso])
        #expect(report.warning(iso) == "The field can change by 0.2 per mm, more than “max slope” (0.05); the preview may have holes.")
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(iso))
        #expect(solid.lipschitz == 4, "the honest bound is the field's slope over the max slope: 0.2 ÷ 0.05")
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/ToImplicitNodeTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorOCCT
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// To Implicit (implicit spec §3.1, §4.4).
struct ToImplicitNodeTests {
    @Test func aSolidBecomesItsSignedDistance() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let toImplicit = h.add(ToImplicitNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.04)])
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: toImplicit, "solid")
        let report = try await h.run([toImplicit])
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(toImplicit))
        #expect(solid.description == "implicit(solid)")
        #expect(abs(solid.distance(0, 0, 0.5) + 0.5) < 0.01 && abs(solid.distance(8, 0, 5) - 3) < 0.01)
        #expect(report.isOK(toImplicit))
        #expect(solid.lipschitz >= 1 && solid.lipschitz < 2)
    }

    @Test func aNamedNodeNamesItsSolid() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let named = h.add(ToImplicitNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.04)])
        h.rename(named, "Bracket")
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: named, "solid")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([named]).implicitSolid(named))
        #expect(solid.description == "implicit(Bracket)")
    }

    @Test func theInspectorFactsAreTheBakeTimeAndMemory() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let toImplicit = h.add(ToImplicitNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.04)])
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: toImplicit, "solid")
        let solid = try #require(try await h.run([toImplicit]).implicitSolid(toImplicit))
        let facts = solid.inspectorFacts
        #expect(facts.map(\.label) == ["Bake time", "Bake memory"])
        #expect(facts[0].value.hasSuffix(" s") && !facts[1].value.isEmpty)
        #expect(ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 3).inspectorFacts.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aToleranceBelowTheMinimumIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let toImplicit = h.add(ToImplicitNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.0005)])
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: toImplicit, "solid")
        #expect(try await h.run([toImplicit]).error(toImplicit) == "“tolerance” must be at least 0.001 mm.")
    }

    @Test func aFilletedPartIsMeasuredToTheKernelsTolerance() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(20, 20, 10)
        let edges = h.add(AllEdgesNode.self)
        let fillet = h.add(FilletNode.self, ["radius": .number(2)])
        let toImplicit = h.add(ToImplicitNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.04)])
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: edges, "solid")
        h.wire(edges, "edges", to: fillet, "edges")
        h.wire(fillet, "solid", to: toImplicit, "solid")
        let report = try await h.run([toImplicit], kernel: kernel)
        let solid = try #require(report.implicitSolid(toImplicit), "\(String(describing: report.state(toImplicit)))")
        // The part is a 20 × 20 × 10 box standing on z = 0, all twelve edges rounded by 2 mm: a rounded box.
        let reference = ImplicitSolid.moved(.box(halfSize: Vector3(10, 10, 5), cornerRadius: 2),
                                            RigidMove(origin: Vector3(0, 0, 5), xAxis: .unitX, yAxis: .unitY, zAxis: .unitZ))
        let random = SeededRandom(seed: 8, stream: 1)
        var near = 0.0, deep = 0.0
        var checked = 0
        for index in 0..<1500 {
            let point = Vector3(random.unit(at: 3 * index) * 30 - 15, random.unit(at: 3 * index + 1) * 30 - 15,
                                random.unit(at: 3 * index + 2) * 20 - 5)
            let exact = reference.signedDistance(at: point)
            guard abs(exact) < 3 else { continue }
            checked += 1
            let difference = abs(solid.signedDistance(at: point) - exact)
            // The tolerance asked for is 0.04 mm: the tessellation is off by that much, and a 0.8 mm grid across a 2 mm fillet by about as
            // much again. Deeper in, near the true distance's ridge, trilinear cuts the ridge short by a little more.
            if abs(exact) < 1.5 { near = max(near, difference) } else { deep = max(deep, difference) }
        }
        #expect(checked > 300)
        #expect(near < 0.2, "worst difference within 1.5 mm of the surface: \(near) mm")
        #expect(deep < 0.3, "worst difference 1.5 to 3 mm out or in: \(deep) mm")
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorEditor/InspectorBuilder+Implicit.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

extension InspectorBuilder {
    /// The "Implicit" section (implicit spec §2.1): the recipe of the implicit solid each implicit output of the node holds, read-only,
    /// and what the payload says about itself (a baked solid's bake time and memory). `nil` for a node with no implicit output. Its
    /// row is labelled "Implicit", or by the socket when there are several; before the node has a result it says "No implicit solid
    /// yet", and when its result holds none (an empty or mixed list) "No implicit solid in this result".
    static func implicitSection(node: Node, registry: NodeRegistry, results: [NodeID: NodeResult]) -> InspectorSectionRows? {
        let outputs = registry.outputs(for: node).filter { $0.type == .implicit }
        guard !outputs.isEmpty else { return nil }
        var rows: [InspectorRow] = []
        for spec in outputs {
            let value = results[node.id]?.outputs?[spec.name]
            let text = value?.implicitSummary()
                ?? (results[node.id]?.outputs == nil ? "No implicit solid yet" : "No implicit solid in this result")
            rows.append(.recipe(label: outputs.count == 1 ? "Implicit" : InspectorLabel.text(for: spec.name), text: text))
            if let only = value?.items.first, value?.items.count == 1, case .implicit(let reference) = only {
                rows += reference.payload.inspectorFacts.map { .readOnly(label: $0.label, text: $0.value) }
            }
        }
        return InspectorSectionRows(title: "Implicit", rows: rows)
    }

    /// The "Mesh" section (implicit spec §5.2): a mesh output's triangle count, resolution, meshing time and memory. `nil` for a node
    /// with no mesh output or no mesh yet.
    static func meshSection(node: Node, registry: NodeRegistry, results: [NodeID: NodeResult]) -> InspectorSectionRows? {
        var rows: [InspectorRow] = []
        for spec in registry.outputs(for: node) where spec.type == .mesh {
            let meshes = (results[node.id]?.outputs?[spec.name]?.items ?? []).compactMap { scalar -> (any MeshPayload)? in
                if case .mesh(let mesh) = scalar { mesh } else { nil }
            }
            if meshes.count == 1, let mesh = meshes.first {
                rows += mesh.inspectorFacts.map { .readOnly(label: $0.label, text: $0.value) }
            } else if meshes.count > 1 {
                rows.append(.readOnly(label: "Meshes", text: "\(meshes.count)"))
            }
        }
        return rows.isEmpty ? nil : InspectorSectionRows(title: "Mesh", rows: rows)
    }
}
````

````swift
/// One line the inspector shows about an implicit solid or a mesh (implicit spec §5.2): "Triangles" and "1,234,000".
public struct PayloadFact: Hashable, Sendable {
    public var label: String
    public var value: String

    public init(_ label: String, _ value: String) {
        self.label = label
        self.value = value
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorGraph/Implicit/Value+Implicit.swift`**

````swift
extension Value {
    /// What a tooltip or the inspector says about the implicit solid this value holds (implicit spec §2.1): the recipe of one solid,
    /// cut to `limit` characters when a limit is given, or "3 implicit solids" for several. `nil` when the value holds anything
    /// else, or nothing.
    public func implicitSummary(limit: Int? = nil) -> String? {
        let solids = items.compactMap { scalar -> ImplicitReference? in if case .implicit(let solid) = scalar { solid } else { nil } }
        guard !solids.isEmpty, solids.count == items.count else { return nil }
        guard solids.count == 1, let only = solids.first else { return "\(solids.count) implicit solids" }
        let text = only.payload.summary
        guard let limit, text.count > limit else { return text }
        return String(text.prefix(max(limit - 1, 0))) + "…"
    }
}
````

````diff
 /// An implicit solid travels along wires and sits in field leaves as an `ImplicitPayload` (CreatorGraph cannot name this type).

+    public var inspectorFacts: [PayloadFact] {
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/Field+Support.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit

extension Field {
    /// The box the field's interesting values lie in, when that is known (implicit spec §3.1: Isosurface takes its bounds "from the
    /// field's support when known"). Only an implicit solid read as a field has one: its surface's box, which a level inside
    /// ±`level` of the surface stays within once grown by it. Everything else (distances, gradients, noise, pictures) goes on forever.
    func support(level: Double) -> BoundingBox? {
        guard case .implicit(let reference) = self else { return nil }
        return ImplicitSolid.grown(reference.payload.payloadBounds, by: abs(level))
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/IsosurfaceNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// Where a field is below a level, as an implicit solid (implicit spec §3.1): inside where field < level, with the value
/// (field − level) ÷ max slope, so the lipschitz bound is 1 when `max slope` is honest. A max slope smaller than the field really
/// changes makes holes in the preview. The solid is clipped to a box: the field's support when it has one, else `bounds`.
public enum IsosurfaceNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.isosurface"
    public static let displayName = "Isosurface"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("field", .field),
        SocketSpec("level", .number, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("max slope", .number, defaultValue: .number(1), range: 0.01...100),
        SocketSpec("bounds", .any, access: .list, optional: true),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Isosurface", controls: [
            .number("level"), .number("max slope"),
            .note("A max slope smaller than the field really changes makes holes in the preview."),
        ]),
    ]
    static let boundsHelp = "Wire a solid, an implicit solid or two corner points into “bounds” to say where to fill."

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let field = try inputs.field("field")
        let (level, maxSlope) = (try inputs.number("level"), try inputs.number("max slope"))
        guard level.isFinite else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“level” isn't a finite number.") }
        guard maxSlope.isFinite, maxSlope > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“max slope” must be greater than 0.") }
        let region = try field.support(level: level) ?? (try bounds(of: inputs))
        guard region.size.x > 0, region.size.y > 0, region.size.z > 0 else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("The region to fill has no volume. \(boundsHelp)")
        }
        let setting = try await ImplicitGrading.make(field, named: "field", over: region, holdsAtZero: false, kernel: kernel,
                                                     context: context)
        var warnings = setting.warnings
        let slope = field.slopeBound.value
        if slope > maxSlope * (1 + 1e-9) {
            let (changes, given) = (slope.millimetreText(locale: .messages), maxSlope.millimetreText(locale: .messages))
            warnings.append("The field can change by \(changes) per mm, more than “max slope” (\(given)); the preview may have holes.")
        }
        let result = ImplicitSolid.isosurface(setting.grading, level: level, maxSlope: maxSlope, region: region)
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(result.reference)], warnings: warnings)
    }

    /// The box the `bounds` input names: a solid's, an implicit solid's, or the box between two points.
    private static func bounds(of inputs: NodeInputs) throws -> BoundingBox {
        guard inputs.has("bounds") else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Isosurface needs a region to fill. \(boundsHelp)") }
        let items = try inputs.list("bounds")
        let boxes = try items.map { item -> BoundingBox in
            switch item {
            case .solid(let solid): return solid.bounds
            case .implicit(let reference): return reference.payload.payloadBounds
            case .vector(let point): return BoundingBox(min: point, max: point)
            default: throw NodeError.invalidValue("“bounds” takes a solid, an implicit solid or two points. \(boundsHelp)")
            }
        }
        guard let first = boxes.first else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Isosurface needs a region to fill. \(boundsHelp)") }
        return boxes.dropFirst().reduce(first) { $0.union($1) }
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/ToImplicitNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// Turns a B-rep solid into its signed distance (implicit spec §3.1, §4.4): the kernel tessellates it at `tolerance`, a BVH measures the
/// distance to the triangles and the sign comes from the triangles' pseudonormals, and the result is baked on a grid of blocks. The
/// bake is cached (`ImplicitBakeCache`), so an edit downstream of this node does not bake again. After this node everything is implicit
/// until Mesh makes triangles.
public enum ToImplicitNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.toImplicit"
    public static let displayName = "To Implicit"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.implicit
    public static let minimumTolerance = 0.001
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("solid", .solid),
        SocketSpec("tolerance", .number, defaultValue: .number(0.01), unit: .millimetres, range: minimumTolerance...1),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "To Implicit", controls: [.slider("tolerance")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let solid = try inputs.solid("solid")
        let tolerance = try inputs.number("tolerance")
        guard tolerance.isFinite, tolerance >= minimumTolerance else {
            let smallest = minimumTolerance.formatted(.number.precision(.fractionLength(0...3)).locale(.messages))
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“tolerance” must be at least \(smallest) mm.")
        }
        let baked: ImplicitBakeCache.Baked
        do {
            baked = try await ImplicitBakeCache.shared.baked(for: solid, tolerance: tolerance, kernel: kernel)
        } catch let error as MeshDistanceError {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue(error.userMessage)
        }
        // A solid named for what it is: the node's own name when the person gave it one.
        let label = context.node.name == displayName ? "solid" : context.node.name
        var warnings: [String] = []
        let wanted = DistanceBaker.spacing(forTolerance: tolerance)
        if baked.spacing > wanted * 1.001 {
            let (used, asked) = (baked.spacing.millimetreText(locale: .messages), wanted.millimetreText(locale: .messages))
            warnings.append("The part is big for this tolerance, so it was measured every \(used) mm instead of every \(asked) mm; "
                + "thin features may be lost.")
        }
        return NodeOutputs(["implicit": .implicit(ImplicitSolid.baked(baked.blocks, label: label).reference)], warnings: warnings)
    }
}
````

**Notes for the implementer:**

- `OutputNode` now has two optional list inputs, `solid` and `mesh`, and two outputs. An Output with nothing wired in used to wait ("Connect or set “solid”."); it now runs and warns "There is nothing to preview or export." See the user decision in the header.
- `PlaceNode`'s `tool` is optional and it gains an optional `implicit` input and an optional `implicits` output. With neither wired it fails with the old message, "Connect or set “tool”." as an error.
- The Mesh node's "preview resolution" is a number where 0 means Auto.
- `ImplicitUndoTests` (CreatorEditorTests) pins that the new inputs undo like any other: a drag of a Mesh resolution is one step named for the input ("Change" and its label), a typed export resolution its own step, and wiring an implicit into Place one "Connect".

**Create `Tests/CreatorEditorTests/ImplicitUndoTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorNodes
import Testing
@testable import CreatorEditor

/// The new inputs of 7c-1 are edited like any other: one undo step each, named by the input's fixed label.
@MainActor
struct ImplicitUndoTests {
    let mesh = testNode(MeshNode.self, id: 1, at: .zero, registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
    let sphere = testNode(ImplicitSphereNode.self, id: 2, at: .zero, registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
    let place = testNode(PlaceNode.self, id: 3, at: .zero, registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)

    private func field(_ editor: EditorModel, socket: SocketName) throws -> InputField {
        let rows = editor.inspectorPage.sections.flatMap(\.rows)
        for row in rows {
            switch row {
            case .slider(let field, _), .number(let field), .integer(let field):
                if field.socket == socket { return field }
            default:
                continue
            }
        }
        throw UndoFixtureError.noField(socket)
    }

    @Test func aPreviewResolutionEditIsOneNamedUndoStep() throws {
        let editor = makeEditor([mesh], registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
        editor.selection = [mesh.id]
        let field = try field(editor, socket: "preview resolution")
        for value in [0.2, 0.3, 0.4] { editor.setInput(field, to: .number(value), continuous: true) }
        #expect(editor.graph.nodes[mesh.id]?.inputValues["preview resolution"] == .number(0.4))
        #expect(editor.document.undoName == "Change \(field.label)")
        editor.document.undo()
        #expect(editor.graph.nodes[mesh.id]?.inputValues["preview resolution"] == nil, "the drag was one step")
        #expect(!editor.document.canUndo)
    }

    @Test func anExportResolutionEditIsItsOwnStep() throws {
        let editor = makeEditor([mesh], registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
        editor.selection = [mesh.id]
        let field = try field(editor, socket: "export resolution")
        editor.setInput(field, to: .number(0.2))
        editor.setInput(field, to: .number(0.3))
        editor.document.undo()
        #expect(editor.graph.nodes[mesh.id]?.inputValues["export resolution"] == .number(0.2))
        editor.document.undo()
        #expect(!editor.document.canUndo)
    }

    @Test func wiringAnImplicitIntoPlaceIsOneStepAndUndoesCleanly() {
        let editor = makeEditor([sphere, place], registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
        editor.connect(wire(sphere, "implicit", place, "implicit"))
        #expect(editor.document.undoName == "Connect")
        #expect(editor.graph.links.count == 1)
        editor.document.undo()
        #expect(editor.graph.links.isEmpty && !editor.document.canUndo)
        editor.document.redo()
        #expect(editor.graph.links.count == 1)
    }
}

private enum UndoFixtureError: Error {
    case noField(SocketName)
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/MeshNodeTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Synchronization
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Mesh (implicit spec §3.6, §4.3) and Output taking meshes (implicit spec §2.3).
struct MeshNodeTests {
    func meshOfASphere(_ h: inout Harness, radius: Double = 6, _ settings: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) -> Node {
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(radius)])
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, settings)
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        return mesh
    }

    @Test func aPreviewIsTheFirstCoarsePassAndRemembersWhatToRefine() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h)
        let report = try await h.run([mesh])
        let made = try #require(report.triangleMesh(mesh))
        // 128 cells across the largest dimension: a 12 mm sphere is meshed every 12 / 128 mm.
        #expect(abs(made.resolution - 12.0 / 128) < 1e-12 && made.quality == .preview)
        let topology = MeshTopology(made)
        #expect(topology.isClosedManifold && topology.eulerCharacteristic == 2)
        let source = try #require(made.source)
        #expect(source.target == .automatic && source.solid.description == "sphere(radius 6 mm)")
        #expect(report.isOK(mesh))
    }

    @Test func aPreviewResolutionCoarserThanTheFirstPassIsTheOnlyPass() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, ["preview resolution": .number(1)])
        let made = try #require(try await h.run([mesh]).triangleMesh(mesh))
        #expect(made.resolution == 1 && made.source?.target == .fixed(1))
    }

    @Test func anExportMeshesAtTheExportResolution() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, ["export resolution": .number(0.5)])
        let report = try await h.runForExport([mesh])
        let made = try #require(report.triangleMesh(mesh))
        #expect(made.resolution == 0.5 && made.quality == .export && made.source == nil)
        #expect(MeshTopology(made).isClosedManifold)
        let exact = 4.0 / 3 * Double.pi * 216
        #expect(abs(made.signedVolume - exact) / exact < 0.01)
    }

    @Test func aPreviewAndAnExportAreNotTheSameResult() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, ["export resolution": .number(0.5)])
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let preview = try await evaluator.evaluate(h.graph, demand: [mesh.id])
        let export = try await evaluator.evaluate(h.graph, demand: [mesh.id], meshQuality: .export)
        #expect(preview.evaluatedNodes.contains(mesh.id) && export.evaluatedNodes.contains(mesh.id))
        #expect(preview.triangleMesh(mesh)?.quality == .preview && export.triangleMesh(mesh)?.quality == .export)
        let again = try await evaluator.evaluate(h.graph, demand: [mesh.id])
        #expect(!again.evaluatedNodes.contains(mesh.id), "the preview is still cached")
    }

    @Test func progressIsReported() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, ["export resolution": .number(0.5)])
        let seen = Mutex<[Double]>([])
        let progress = MeshProgress { value in seen.withLock { $0.append(value) } }
        _ = try await Evaluator(registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
            .evaluate(h.graph, demand: [mesh.id], meshQuality: .export, progress: progress)
        let values = seen.withLock { $0 }
        #expect(values.first == 0 && values.last == 1 && values == values.sorted())
    }

    @Test func theInspectorFactsAreTheCountTheResolutionTheTimeAndTheMemory() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, ["export resolution": .number(0.5)])
        let made = try #require(try await h.runForExport([mesh]).triangleMesh(mesh))
        let facts = made.inspectorFacts
        #expect(facts.map(\.label) == ["Triangles", "Resolution", "Meshing time", "Memory"])
        #expect(facts[0].value == made.triangleCount.formatted(.number.locale(.messages)) && facts[1].value == "0.5 mm")
        #expect(facts[2].value.hasSuffix(" s"))
        let preview = try #require(try await h.run([mesh]).triangleMesh(mesh))
        #expect(preview.inspectorFacts[1].value == "Preview 0.094 mm")
    }

    @Test func settingsAreChecked() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let coarse = meshOfASphere(&h, ["export resolution": .number(0.005)])
        let negative = meshOfASphere(&h, ["preview resolution": .number(-1)])
        let report = try await h.run([coarse, negative])
        #expect(report.error(coarse) == "“export resolution” must be at least 0.01 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(negative) == "“preview resolution” must be 0 (Auto) or more.")
    }

    @Test func aPartFarFromTheOriginWarnsAboutSinglePrecision() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let far = meshOfASphere(&h, radius: 12_000, ["preview resolution": .number(5_000)])
        let near = meshOfASphere(&h, radius: 12)
        let report = try await h.run([far, near])
        #expect(report.warning(far) == "This part reaches 12,000 mm from the origin; beyond 10,000 mm the GPU's single-precision "
            + "coordinates blur its surface.")
        #expect(report.isOK(near))
    }

    @Test func aShapeTooSmallToMeshIsRefusedWithAMessage() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h, radius: 0.0001, ["preview resolution": .number(5)])
        #expect(try await h.run([mesh]).error(mesh) == ImplicitMeshingError.empty.userMessage)
    }

    @Test func anExportTooBigForMemoryIsRefusedWithTheSpecsMessage() async throws {
        // Physical memory is whatever this Mac has, so ask for an absurd resolution of a big part.
        var h = Harness()
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(1000)])
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, ["export resolution": .number(0.01)])
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        let message = try #require(try await h.runForExport([mesh]).error(mesh))
        #expect(message.hasPrefix("This needs about ") && message.contains(" GB at 0.01 mm; coarsen to "))
        #expect(message.hasSuffix(" mm or split the part."))
    }

    // MARK: Output

    @Test func anOutputTakesMeshesAndKeepsTheSolidsApart() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let mesh = meshOfASphere(&h)
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self, output: true)
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: output, "mesh")
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: output, "solid")
        let report = try await h.run([output])
        #expect(report.value(output, "solid")?.solids?.count == 1)
        #expect(report.value(output, "mesh")?.triangleMeshes?.count == 1)
        #expect(report.isOK(output))
    }

    @Test func anImplicitWiredIntoAnOutputsMeshSocketIsMeshedAtTheDefaultSettings() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(1)])
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self, output: true)
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: output, "mesh")
        let preview = try #require(try await h.run([output]).value(output, "mesh")?.triangleMeshes?.first)
        #expect(preview.quality == .preview && preview.resolution == PreviewSchedule.finest, "2 / 128 mm is finer than a preview goes")
        let export = try #require(try await h.runForExport([output]).value(output, "mesh")?.triangleMeshes?.first)
        #expect(export.quality == .export && export.resolution == MeshNode.defaultExportResolution)
        #expect(MeshTopology(export).isClosedManifold)
    }

    @Test func aListOfImplicitsMakesAListOfMeshes() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let radii = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["start": .number(2), "step": .number(2), "count": .integer(2)])
        let spheres = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self, output: true)
        h.wire(radii, "values", to: spheres, "radius")
        h.wire(spheres, "implicit", to: output, "mesh")
        let meshes = try #require(try await h.run([output]).value(output, "mesh")?.triangleMeshes)
        #expect(meshes.count == 2 && meshes[1].signedVolume > meshes[0].signedVolume)
    }

    @Test func aSolidCannotBeWiredIntoAMeshSocket() {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self, output: true)
        let problem = h.graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: box.id, socket: "solid"),
                                                to: Endpoint(node: output.id, socket: "mesh"), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry)
        #expect(problem == .typeMismatch(from: .solid, to: .mesh))
    }

    @Test func anImplicitCanBeWiredIntoAMeshSocketAndAMeshIntoNothingElse() {
        var h = Harness()
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self)
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self, output: true)
        let graph = h.graph
        let registry = BuiltInNodes.registry
        #expect(graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: sphere.id, socket: "implicit"),
                                        to: Endpoint(node: output.id, socket: "mesh"), registry: registry) == nil)
        #expect(graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: mesh.id, socket: "mesh"),
                                        to: Endpoint(node: output.id, socket: "solid"), registry: registry)
            == .typeMismatch(from: .mesh, to: .solid))
        #expect(graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: mesh.id, socket: "mesh"),
                                        to: Endpoint(node: sphere.id, socket: "radius"), registry: registry)
            == .typeMismatch(from: .mesh, to: .number))
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/PlaceImplicitTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Place accepting implicit solids (implicit spec §3.3): a rigid move per placement, exact.
struct PlaceImplicitTests {
    /// A cylinder tool (radius 2, height 10, centred on the origin) and Grid Points → Points to Placements → Place.
    struct Fixture {
        var h = Harness()
        let tool: Node, placements: Node, place: Node

        init(columns: Int = 2, rows: Int = 1, direction: Vector3 = .unitZ) {
            tool = h.add(ImplicitCylinderNode.self, ["radius": .number(2), "height": .number(10)])
            let grid = h.add(GridPointsNode.self, ["countX": .integer(columns), "countY": .integer(rows), "spacingX": .number(20),
                                                   "spacingY": .number(20),
            ])
            placements = h.add(PointsToPlacementsNode.self, ["direction": .vector(direction)])
            place = h.add(PlaceNode.self)
            h.wire(grid, "points", to: placements, "points")
            h.wire(placements, "placements", to: place, "placements")
            h.wire(tool, "implicit", to: place, "implicit")
        }
    }

    @Test func aCopyOfTheImplicitSitsAtEachPlacement() async throws {
        let f = Fixture()
        let report = try await f.h.run([f.place])
        let copies = try #require(report.value(f.place, "implicits")?.implicitSolids)
        #expect(copies.count == 2 && report.isOK(f.place))
        // Grid points at x = −10 and 10, facing +Z: the cylinder stands there, 5 mm each way along its axis.
        #expect(copies[0].distance(-10, 0, 0) == -2 && copies[1].distance(10, 0, 0) == -2)
        #expect(copies[0].distance(-10, 0, 5) == 0 && copies[1].distance(10, 0, -5) == 0)
        #expect(copies[0].distance(10, 0, 0) == 18, "the copy at −10 is 20 mm from the other cylinder's axis, less its radius")
        #expect(report.value(f.place, "solids") == nil)
    }

    @Test func aPlacementFacingAwayTurnsTheShapeToFaceIt() async throws {
        let f = Fixture(columns: 1, rows: 1, direction: .unitX)
        let copy = try #require(try await f.h.run([f.place]).value(f.place, "implicits")?.implicitSolids?.first)
        // Standing along +Z becomes lying along +X: the axis runs along X, the cylinder 5 mm each way, so x = 4 is 1 mm inside its end.
        #expect(abs(copy.distance(4, 0, 0) + 1) < 1e-9 && abs(copy.distance(0, 0, 4) - 2) < 1e-9)
    }

    @Test func movingIsExact() async throws {
        let f = Fixture()
        let copies = try #require(try await f.h.run([f.place]).value(f.place, "implicits")?.implicitSolids)
        for copy in copies { #expect(copy.lipschitz == 1) }
        #expect(copies[0].description == "place(cylinder(radius 2 mm, height 10 mm))")
    }

    @Test func aSolidToolStillWorksAndBothCanBePlacedTogether() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        let box = f.h.box(10, 10, 10)
        f.h.wire(box, "solid", to: f.place, "tool")
        let report = try await f.h.run([f.place])
        #expect(report.value(f.place, "solids")?.solids?.count == 2)
        #expect(report.value(f.place, "implicits")?.implicitSolids?.count == 2)
    }

    @Test func aPlaceNeedsAToolOrAnImplicit() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let grid = h.add(GridPointsNode.self)
        let placements = h.add(PointsToPlacementsNode.self)
        let place = h.add(PlaceNode.self)
        h.wire(grid, "points", to: placements, "points")
        h.wire(placements, "placements", to: place, "placements")
        #expect(try await h.run([place]).error(place) == "Connect or set “tool”.")
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/MeshInput.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel

/// The meshes a mesh socket holds. An implicit solid wired into a mesh socket is meshed at the Mesh node's default settings
/// (implicit spec §2.3), by the node that takes it: `Scalar.converted(to: .mesh)` leaves it as it is, because meshing is work that
/// needs the run's quality and can be cancelled.
enum MeshInput {
    /// Each scalar as a mesh, in order; the warnings are those of meshing the implicit ones.
    static func meshes(_ scalars: [Scalar], socket: SocketName,
                       context: EvalContext) async throws -> (meshes: [TriangleMesh], warnings: [String]) {
        var meshes: [TriangleMesh] = []
        var warnings: [String] = []
        for scalar in scalars {
            switch scalar {
            case .mesh(let payload):
                guard let mesh = payload as? TriangleMesh else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch(socket, expected: .mesh) }
                meshes.append(mesh)
            case .implicit(let reference):
                guard let solid = reference.solid else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch(socket, expected: .mesh) }
                let made = try await MeshNode.mesh(solid, exportResolution: MeshNode.defaultExportResolution, previewResolution: 0,
                                                   context: context)
                meshes.append(made.mesh)
                warnings += made.warnings
            default:
                throw NodeError.typeMismatch(socket, expected: .mesh)
            }
        }
        return (meshes, warnings)
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/MeshNode.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation

/// Makes triangles from an implicit solid (implicit spec §3.6, §4.3): marching cubes over the sparse bricks the surface passes through,
/// watertight and manifold, vertices snapped to the surface. The result depends on the run (`EvalContext.meshQuality`):
/// - **Preview** (the viewport): the first pass, at 128 cells across the largest dimension, or at `preview resolution` if that is
///   coarser. The mesh remembers the solid and the target (`TriangleMesh.source`), and the app refines it in the background, pass by
///   pass, until the preview resolution (Auto: the finest that meshes in about a second).
/// - **Export**: `export resolution`, once, with a memory estimate first.
public enum MeshNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.mesh"
    public static let displayName = "Mesh"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.mesh
    public static let readsMeshQuality = true
    /// The default settings an implicit wired into a mesh socket is meshed with (implicit spec §2.3).
    public static let defaultExportResolution = 0.1
    public static let minimumExportResolution = 0.01
    /// How far from the origin a part may reach before the warning about single-precision coordinates (implicit spec §4.2), in mm.
    static let precisionLimit = 10_000.0
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("implicit", .implicit),
        SocketSpec("export resolution", .number, defaultValue: .number(defaultExportResolution), unit: .millimetres,
                   range: minimumExportResolution...2),
        SocketSpec("preview resolution", .number, defaultValue: .number(0), unit: .millimetres, range: 0...5),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("mesh", .mesh)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Mesh", controls: [
            .number("export resolution"), .number("preview resolution"),
            .note("A preview resolution of 0 is Auto: the finest that meshes in about a second."),
        ]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let solid = try inputs.implicitSolid("implicit")
        let (export, preview) = (try inputs.number("export resolution"), try inputs.number("preview resolution"))
        guard export.isFinite, export >= minimumExportResolution else {
            let smallest = minimumExportResolution.millimetreText(locale: .messages)
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“export resolution” must be at least \(smallest) mm.")
        }
        guard preview.isFinite, preview >= 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“preview resolution” must be 0 (Auto) or more.") }
        let made = try await mesh(solid, exportResolution: export, previewResolution: preview, context: context)
        return NodeOutputs(["mesh": .mesh(made.mesh)], warnings: made.warnings)
    }

    /// The mesh of `solid` for this run, and what is worth saying about how it was made. `previewResolution` 0 is Auto.
    static func mesh(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, exportResolution: Double, previewResolution: Double,
                     context: EvalContext) async throws -> (mesh: TriangleMesh, warnings: [String]) {
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: solid, choice: context.implicitEngine)
        let mesher = ImplicitMesher(evaluator: selection.evaluator)
        var warnings = selection.warning.map { [$0] } ?? []
        // The GPU works in single precision (implicit spec §4.2): a part this far out is blurred.
        let box = solid.bounds
        let farthest = [box.min, box.max].flatMap { [abs($0.x), abs($0.y), abs($0.z)] }.max() ?? 0
        if farthest > precisionLimit {
            let reach = farthest.formatted(.number.precision(.fractionLength(0)).locale(.messages))
            warnings.append("This part reaches \(reach) mm from the origin; beyond 10,000 mm the GPU's single-precision "
                + "coordinates blur its surface.")
        }
        let progress = context.meshProgress
        do {
            switch context.meshQuality {
            case .export:
                let mesh = try await mesher.mesh(solid, resolution: exportResolution, quality: .export) { progress($0) }
                return (mesh, warnings)
            case .preview:
                let target: PreviewSchedule.Target = previewResolution > 0 ? .fixed(previewResolution) : .automatic
                let first = PreviewSchedule(target).firstResolution(for: solid.bounds)
                let mesh = try await mesher.mesh(solid, resolution: first, quality: .preview,
                                                 source: MeshSource(solid: solid, target: target)) { progress($0) }
                return (mesh, warnings)
            }
        } catch let error as ImplicitMeshingError {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue(error.userMessage)
        }
    }
}
````

````diff
+
+    /// The implicit solid for each placement, moved onto it (implicit spec §3.3): a rigid transform, so the distance stays exact.
+    private static func placedImplicits(_ inputs: NodeInputs) throws -> DataTree {
+        let grid = try InstanceGrid(inputs, ["placements", "implicit"])
+        guard grid.count > 0 else { return grid.tree(of: []) }
+        let planes = try grid.planes("placements")
+        let shapes = try grid.implicits("implicit")
+        var copies: [Scalar] = []
+        for (index, (plane, shape)) in zip(planes, shapes).enumerated() {
+            guard let move = RigidMove(plane: plane) else {
+                throw NodeError.invalidValue("Placement \(InstancePath.text(grid.items[index])) has no usable direction: "
+                    + "its normal and x axis must not be zero or along each other.")
+            }
+            guard let solid = shape.solid else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch("implicit", expected: .implicit) }
+            copies.append(.implicit(ImplicitSolid.moved(solid, move).reference))
+        }
+        return grid.tree(of: copies)
     }
 }
````

### Task 14: Meshes in the viewport: display solids, the progressive preview and the badge

Show a mesh as a viewport item, refine it pass by pass in the background, stop the passes when an edit replaces the mesh, and put the "Preview 0.4 mm" badge (with a spinner while refining) in the corner.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Package.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppLayout.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Scene.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppRoot.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/DocumentParts.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshBadgeDock.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshBadgeView.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshRefinementModel.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshScene.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshSolids.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/TriangleMesh+Display.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/SceneBuilder.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorKernel/DisplayMeshStorage.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorViewport/Scene/TessellationCache.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/MetalCreatorApp/main.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/AppMeshPreviewTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshBadgeWindowTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshFixture.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshRefinementModelTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshSceneTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorKernelTests/DisplayMeshStorageTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorViewportTests/MeshOnDisplayTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `TriangleMesh`, `MeshSource`, `PreviewSchedule`, `ImplicitMesher`, `ImplicitEvaluators` (Tasks 2, 7, 9); `MeshNode` (Task 13).
- Produces (the public declarations this task adds; later tasks use these exact names):

````swift
public final class DisplayMeshStorage: SolidStorage
public let mesh: DisplayMesh
public init(_ mesh: DisplayMesh)
public var estimatedBytes: Int
public convenience init(displaying mesh: DisplayMesh)
public var displayedMesh: DisplayMesh?
````


**Notes for the implementer:**

- A mesh is shown as a `Solid` whose storage is a `DisplayMesh` (CreatorKernel's `DisplayMeshStorage`): the viewport already caches and draws solids, so only `TessellationCache` changed. `MeshSolids` keeps one display solid per mesh so the viewport does not reload it on every scene rebuild.
- `MeshRefinementModel.follow` is called from `AppModel+Scene` with the preview meshes of the shown nodes; a mesh no longer there has its task cancelled. `AppModel` takes `implicitEngine:` (default `.cpu`; `MetalCreatorApp` passes `.automatic`).
- `AppModel.settle()` does not wait for refinement (tests would wait for every pass); `meshRefinement.waitForRefinement()` does.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/AppMeshPreviewTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// A mesh in the app (implicit spec §5.1, §4.3): it appears at once as the first pass, gets finer in the background, and an edit starts
/// the passes over for the new shape.
@MainActor
struct AppMeshPreviewTests {
    /// Sphere → Mesh → Output, previewed down to 0.06 mm.
    func graph(radius: Double = 6, preview: Double = 0.06) -> (graph: Graph, sphere: Node, mesh: Node, output: Node) {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let sphere = builder.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(radius)])
        let mesh = builder.add(MeshNode.self, ["preview resolution": .number(preview)])
        let output = builder.add(OutputNode.self)
        builder.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        builder.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: output, "mesh")
        return (builder.graph, sphere, mesh, output)
    }

    /// How many triangles the viewport's one item has.
    func triangles(_ app: AppModel) -> Int? {
        app.viewport.items.first?.solid.displayedMesh.map { $0.indices.count / 3 }
    }

    @Test func theFirstPassIsShownAtOnceAndRefinesInTheBackground() async throws {
        let fixture = graph()
        let app = await makeApp(fixture.graph)
        #expect(app.viewport.items.count == 1 && app.viewport.items[0].solid.displayedMesh != nil)
        let coarse = try #require(triangles(app))
        #expect(app.meshRefinement.badge == .init(text: "Preview 0.094 mm", isRefining: true))
        await app.meshRefinement.waitForRefinement()
        await app.settle()
        let fine = try #require(triangles(app))
        #expect(fine > coarse, "the finer pass replaced the coarse one: \(coarse) → \(fine) triangles")
        #expect(app.meshRefinement.badge == .init(text: "Preview 0.06 mm", isRefining: false))
        #expect(app.viewport.items.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func anEditStopsThePassesForTheOldShapeAndStartsTheNewOne() async throws {
        let fixture = graph()
        let app = await makeApp(fixture.graph)
        let before = Set(app.meshRefinement.statuses.keys)
        try app.document.perform(.setInput(fixture.sphere.id, "radius", .number(5)))
        await app.settle()
        let after = Set(app.meshRefinement.statuses.keys)
        #expect(before.count == 1 && after.count == 1 && before.isDisjoint(with: after), "the old mesh is forgotten")
        await app.meshRefinement.waitForRefinement()
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.meshRefinement.badge?.isRefining == false)
        let mesh = try #require(app.viewport.items.first?.solid.displayedMesh)
        let bounds = try #require(BoundingBox(points: mesh.positions))
        #expect(abs(bounds.max.x - 5) < 0.06, "the finest pass is the new radius")
    }

    @Test func nothingIsRefinedWithoutAMesh() async {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        _ = builder.box()
        let app = await makeApp(builder.graph)
        #expect(app.meshRefinement.badge == nil && app.meshRefinement.statuses.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func openingAnotherDocumentStopsTheRefinement() async throws {
        let fixture = graph()
        let app = await makeApp(fixture.graph)
        app.load(GraphFile(), from: nil)
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.meshRefinement.badge == nil && app.meshRefinement.refined.isEmpty)
        #expect(app.viewport.items.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aMeshPreviewDoesNotDependOnTheKernel() async throws {
        let fixture = graph(preview: 1)
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        await kernel.clearLog()
        let app = await makeApp(fixture.graph, kernel: kernel)
        #expect(app.viewport.items.count == 1)
        #expect(await !kernel.operationLog.contains("tessellate"))
    }

    @Test func theSceneSourceOfAMeshIsItsOutputSocket() async throws {
        let fixture = graph(preview: 1)
        let app = await makeApp(fixture.graph)
        let solid = try #require(app.viewport.items.first?.solid)
        #expect(app.sources[ObjectIdentifier(solid)] == Endpoint(node: fixture.output.id, socket: "mesh"))
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshBadgeWindowTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorNodes
import MetalUI
import MetalUITesting
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// The "Preview 0.4 mm" badge in the app's real window (implicit spec §5.1). How it looks is a human check (group IM).
@MainActor
struct MeshBadgeWindowTests {
    func open(preview: Double) async throws -> AppWindow {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let sphere = builder.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(6)])
        let mesh = builder.add(MeshNode.self, ["preview resolution": .number(preview)])
        let output = builder.add(OutputNode.self)
        builder.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        builder.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: output, "mesh")
        return try await AppWindow.open(builder.graph)
    }

    func texts(_ text: String, in app: AppWindow) throws -> Int {
        try app.window.elements { $0.label == text || $0.value == text }.count
    }

    @Test func aMeshPreviewShowsItsBadge() async throws {
        let app = try await open(preview: 1)
        try await app.window.runUntilIdle()
        #expect(app.model.meshRefinement.badge == .init(text: "Preview 1 mm", isRefining: false))
        #expect(try texts("Preview 1 mm", in: app) >= 1)
    }

    @Test func theBadgeFollowsTheRefinementAndKeepsItsLastResolution() async throws {
        let app = try await open(preview: 0.06)
        await app.model.meshRefinement.waitForRefinement()
        await app.model.settle()
        try await app.window.runUntilIdle()
        #expect(try texts("Preview 0.06 mm", in: app) >= 1)
        #expect(try texts("Preview 0.094 mm", in: app) == 0)
    }

    @Test func noMeshNoBadge() async throws {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        _ = builder.box()
        let app = try await AppWindow.open(builder.graph)
        #expect(app.model.meshRefinement.badge == nil)
        #expect(try app.window.elements { ($0.label ?? "").hasPrefix("Preview ") }.isEmpty)
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshRefinementModelTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// The progressive preview (implicit spec §4.3, §5.1): passes arrive finer and finer, and an edit stops them.
@MainActor
struct MeshRefinementModelTests {
    /// The first pass of a sphere of radius 6, as the Mesh node makes it, with a target.
    func firstPass(of solid: ImplicitSolid = .sphere(radius: 6), target: PreviewSchedule.Target) async throws -> TriangleMesh {
        let first = PreviewSchedule(target).firstResolution(for: solid.bounds)
        return try await ImplicitMesher(evaluator: CPUBatchEvaluator(solid))
            .mesh(solid, resolution: first, quality: .preview, source: MeshSource(solid: solid, target: target))
    }

    @Test func passesArriveFinerUntilTheTarget() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let base = try await firstPass(target: .fixed(0.06))
        model.follow([base])
        #expect(model.badge == .init(text: "Preview 0.094 mm", isRefining: true))
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        let pass = try #require(model.refined[base.id])
        #expect(pass.resolution == 0.06 && pass.triangleCount > base.triangleCount)
        #expect(MeshTopology(pass).isClosedManifold)
        #expect(model.badge == .init(text: "Preview 0.06 mm", isRefining: false))
        #expect(model.revision >= 2)
    }

    @Test func aMeshAtItsTargetIsNeverRefined() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let base = try await firstPass(target: .fixed(1))
        model.follow([base])
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        #expect(model.refined.isEmpty)
        #expect(model.badge == .init(text: "Preview 1 mm", isRefining: false))
    }

    @Test func aMeshThatLeavesTheSceneStopsItsPassesAndIsForgotten() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let base = try await firstPass(target: .fixed(0.06))
        model.follow([base])
        model.follow([])
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        #expect(model.refined.isEmpty && model.statuses.isEmpty && model.badge == nil)
    }

    @Test func aReplacementMeshStartsOverAndTheOldOneIsDropped() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let old = try await firstPass(target: .fixed(0.06))
        model.follow([old])
        let replacement = try await firstPass(of: .sphere(radius: 5), target: .fixed(0.06))
        model.follow([replacement])
        #expect(model.statuses.keys.contains(replacement.id) && !model.statuses.keys.contains(old.id))
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        #expect(model.refined[old.id] == nil && model.refined[replacement.id] != nil)
    }

    @Test func followingTheSameMeshAgainDoesNotRestartIt() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let base = try await firstPass(target: .fixed(0.06))
        model.follow([base])
        let revision = model.revision
        model.follow([base])
        #expect(model.revision == revision)
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        let done = try #require(model.refined[base.id])
        model.follow([base])
        #expect(model.refined[base.id]?.id == done.id, "a finished mesh is not meshed again")
    }

    @Test func aMeshWithoutASourceIsIgnored() {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        model.follow([MeshFixture.tetrahedron])
        #expect(model.statuses.isEmpty && model.badge == nil)
    }

    @Test func theBadgeShowsTheCoarsestOfSeveralMeshes() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        let coarse = try await firstPass(of: .sphere(radius: 12), target: .fixed(1))
        let fine = try await firstPass(of: .sphere(radius: 3), target: .fixed(1))
        model.follow([coarse, fine])
        #expect(model.badge?.text == "Preview 1 mm")
    }

    @Test func resetStopsEverything() async throws {
        let model = MeshRefinementModel(engine: .cpu)
        model.follow([try await firstPass(target: .fixed(0.06))])
        model.reset()
        await model.waitForRefinement()
        #expect(model.refined.isEmpty && model.badge == nil)
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorAppTests/MeshPreview/MeshSceneTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// Meshes in the scene (implicit spec §5.1): the display mesh, the display solid, and what the scene builder does with a mesh result.
struct MeshSceneTests {
    func tetrahedron(id: UUID = UUID(), source: MeshSource? = nil) -> TriangleMesh {
        TriangleMesh(positions: MeshFixture.tetrahedron.positions, triangles: MeshFixture.tetrahedron.triangles, resolution: 0.5,
                     source: source, id: id)
    }

    @Test func theDisplayMeshAveragesTheFacesWhenTheMesherGaveNoNormals() {
        let display = tetrahedron().displayMesh
        #expect(display.positions.count == 4 && display.indices.count == 12 && display.triangleFaces.count == 4)
        #expect(display.triangleFaces.allSatisfy { $0 == FaceID(0) } && display.edgePolylines.isEmpty)
        // A regular tetrahedron about the origin: each vertex normal points away from the centre.
        for (position, normal) in zip(display.positions, display.normals) {
            #expect(abs(normal.length - 1) < 1e-9 && normal.dot(position) > 0)
        }
    }

    @Test func theMeshersNormalsAreUsed() {
        var mesh = tetrahedron()
        mesh.normals = [SIMD3<Float>](repeating: SIMD3(0, 0, 1), count: 4)
        #expect(mesh.displayMesh.normals.allSatisfy { $0 == Vector3.unitZ })
    }

    @Test func aMeshKeepsOneDisplaySolidWhileItIsShown() {
        let solids = MeshSolids()
        let mesh = tetrahedron()
        let first = solids.solid(for: mesh)
        #expect(solids.solid(for: mesh) === first)
        #expect(solids.solid(for: tetrahedron()) !== first, "another mesh, another solid")
        solids.keep([mesh.id])
        #expect(solids.count == 1 && solids.solid(for: mesh) === first)
        solids.keep([])
        #expect(solids.count == 0)
    }

    @Test func aMeshResultMakesAnItemWithTheNodeAsItsSource() {
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(MeshNode.typeID)
        let mesh = tetrahedron()
        let scene = MeshScene(solids: MeshSolids())
        let items = SceneBuilder.items(of: node, outputs: ["mesh": .one(.mesh(mesh))], isGhost: false, graph: Graph(), meshes: scene)
        #expect(items.count == 1 && items[0].source == Endpoint(node: node.id, socket: "mesh"))
        #expect(items[0].item.solid.displayedMesh?.indices == mesh.displayMesh.indices)
        #expect(!items[0].item.isGhost)
    }

    @Test func aRefinementReplacesTheMeshItShows() {
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(MeshNode.typeID)
        let base = tetrahedron(), finer = tetrahedron()
        var scene = MeshScene(solids: MeshSolids())
        scene.refined = [base.id: finer]
        let items = SceneBuilder.items(of: node, outputs: ["mesh": .one(.mesh(base))], isGhost: false, graph: Graph(), meshes: scene)
        let shown = scene.solids.solid(for: finer)
        #expect(items[0].item.solid === shown)
    }

    @Test func aStaleMeshIsAGhostLikeAStaleSolid() {
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(MeshNode.typeID)
        let mesh = tetrahedron()
        let options = SceneBuilder.Options(selection: [], showsGuides: false, meshes: MeshScene(solids: MeshSolids()))
        let items = SceneBuilder.scene(shown: [node.id], graph: Graph(nodes: [node.id: node]), results: [:],
                                       lastGood: [node.id: ["mesh": .one(.mesh(mesh))]], options: options)
        #expect(items.count == 1 && items[0].item.isGhost)
    }

    @Test func onlyCurrentMeshesWithASourceAreForRefinement() {
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(MeshNode.typeID)
        let source = MeshSource(solid: .sphere(radius: 3), target: .automatic)
        let refinable = tetrahedron(source: source), plain = tetrahedron()
        let current = NodeResult(state: .ok(duration: .zero), outputs: ["mesh": .list([.mesh(refinable), .mesh(plain)])])
        #expect(SceneBuilder.previewMeshes(shown: [node.id], results: [node.id: current]).map(\.id) == [refinable.id])
        let failed = NodeResult(state: .error("e"), outputs: ["mesh": .one(.mesh(refinable))])
        #expect(SceneBuilder.previewMeshes(shown: [node.id], results: [node.id: failed]).isEmpty)
        #expect(SceneBuilder.previewMeshes(shown: [node.id], results: [:]).isEmpty)
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorKernelTests/DisplayMeshStorageTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Testing
@testable import CreatorKernel

/// A solid that is only a mesh on display (implicit spec §5.1).
struct DisplayMeshStorageTests {
    let mesh = DisplayMesh(
        positions: [Vector3(0, 0, 0), Vector3(4, 0, 0), Vector3(0, 3, 0), Vector3(0, 0, 2)],
        normals: [Vector3](repeating: .unitZ, count: 4), indices: [0, 2, 1, 0, 1, 3, 0, 3, 2, 1, 2, 3],
        triangleFaces: [FaceID](repeating: FaceID(0), count: 4), edgePolylines: [:])

    @Test func theSolidHasBoundsAroundTheVerticesAndNoTopology() {
        let solid = Solid(displaying: mesh)
        #expect(solid.bounds == BoundingBox(min: .zero, max: Vector3(4, 3, 2)))
        #expect(solid.topology.faces.isEmpty && solid.topology.edges.isEmpty)
        #expect(solid.estimatedBytes == solid.storage.estimatedBytes)
    }

    @Test func theMeshComesBack() {
        let solid = Solid(displaying: mesh)
        #expect(solid.displayedMesh?.indices == mesh.indices && solid.displayedMesh?.positions == mesh.positions)
    }

    @Test func aSolidFromAKernelIsNotAMeshOnDisplay() async throws {
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        let box = try await kernel.extrude(.rectangle(width: 2, height: 2, plane: .xy), distance: 2, mode: .oneSided,
                                           tag: NodeTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0))
        #expect(box.displayedMesh == nil)
    }

    @Test func aMeshWithNoVerticesHasZeroBounds() {
        let empty = Solid(displaying: DisplayMesh(positions: [], normals: [], indices: [], triangleFaces: [], edgePolylines: [:]))
        #expect(empty.bounds == BoundingBox(min: .zero, max: .zero))
    }

    @Test func eachMeshOnDisplayIsItsOwnSolid() {
        #expect(Solid(displaying: mesh) !== Solid(displaying: mesh))
    }
}
````

**Create `Tests/CreatorViewportTests/MeshOnDisplayTests.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorViewport

/// A mesh the host built is drawn as it is (implicit spec §5.1): the kernel is never asked to tessellate it.
@MainActor
struct MeshOnDisplayTests {
    let mesh = DisplayMesh(
        positions: [Vector3(0, 0, 0), Vector3(4, 0, 0), Vector3(0, 3, 0), Vector3(0, 0, 2)],
        normals: [Vector3](repeating: .unitZ, count: 4), indices: [0, 2, 1, 0, 1, 3, 0, 3, 2, 1, 2, 3],
        triangleFaces: [FaceID](repeating: FaceID(0), count: 4), edgePolylines: [:])

    @Test func theCacheKeepsAMeshOnDisplayWithoutTheKernel() async throws {
        let kernel = StubMeshKernel()
        let cache = TessellationCache()
        let shown = Solid(displaying: mesh)
        try await cache.load([shown], tolerance: 0.05, kernel: kernel)
        #expect(await kernel.tessellations == 0 && cache.tessellationCount == 0)
        #expect(cache.mesh(for: shown)?.mesh.indices == mesh.indices)
    }

    @Test func aSceneWithAMeshAndAPartLoadsBoth() async throws {
        let kernel = StubMeshKernel()
        let model = ViewportModel(kernel: kernel, pose: nil)
        model.viewSize = ViewportSize(width: 400, height: 300)
        let part = try await fakeBox()
        let shown = Solid(displaying: mesh)
        model.show([ViewportItem(solid: part), ViewportItem(solid: shown)])
        await model.waitForMeshes()
        #expect(model.items.count == 2 && model.meshError == nil)
        #expect(await kernel.tessellations == 1, "only the part")
        #expect(model.displayMesh(of: shown)?.positions == mesh.positions)
        #expect(model.sceneBounds?.max.x == max(4, part.bounds.max.x))
    }

    @Test func hoveringAMeshWithNoFacesDoesNotTrouble() async throws {
        let model = ViewportModel(kernel: StubMeshKernel(), pose: nil)
        model.viewSize = ViewportSize(width: 400, height: 300)
        model.show([ViewportItem(solid: Solid(displaying: mesh))])
        await model.waitForMeshes()
        for x in stride(from: 50.0, to: 350.0, by: 50) {
            model.pointerHovered(at: ScreenPoint(x, 150))
        }
        #expect(model.meshError == nil)
    }
}
````

**Create `Sources/CreatorApp/MeshPreview/MeshRefinementModel.swift`**

````swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import Foundation
import Observation

/// The progressive preview of meshes (implicit spec §4.3): the evaluator hands the viewport the first, coarse pass of each Mesh node
/// (`TriangleMesh.source` remembers the implicit and the target), and this meshes the same implicit again with the voxel size halved,
/// pass after pass, until the preview resolution (Auto: the finest that meshes in under a second, estimated from the pass before).
/// Each pass replaces the mesh on screen (`refined`), and an edit cancels the passes running for the meshes it replaced: a mesh that
/// is no longer in the scene (`follow`) has its pass stopped, so the machine works for the shape that is on screen.
@MainActor
@Observable
final class MeshRefinementModel {
    /// What the badge says: the coarsest resolution on screen, and whether some mesh is still being refined.
    struct Badge: Equatable {
        var text: String
        var isRefining: Bool
    }

    /// A mesh's progress.
    struct Status: Equatable {
        var resolution: Double
        var isRefining: Bool
    }

    /// The finest pass so far for each mesh being followed, by the ID of the mesh the evaluator made.
    private(set) var refined: [UUID: TriangleMesh] = [:]
    private(set) var statuses: [UUID: Status] = [:]
    /// Changes whenever a pass arrives or a mesh goes, so the scene follows.
    private(set) var revision = 0

    @ObservationIgnored private let engine: ImplicitEngineChoice
    @ObservationIgnored private var tasks: [UUID: Task<Void, Never>] = [:]

    init(engine: ImplicitEngineChoice) {
        self.engine = engine
    }

    /// The badge for the meshes on screen: "Preview 0.4 mm" (the coarsest of them) with a spinner while any is refining; `nil` with
    /// none.
    var badge: Badge? {
        guard let coarsest = statuses.values.map(\.resolution).max() else { return nil }
        let size = coarsest.formatted(.number.precision(.fractionLength(0...3)).locale(.messages))
        return Badge(text: "Preview \(size) mm", isRefining: statuses.values.contains { $0.isRefining })
    }

    /// Follows `meshes`, the preview meshes now in the scene: stops the passes of meshes that have gone, forgets their refinements,
    /// and starts refining the new ones. A mesh already followed is left alone.
    func follow(_ meshes: [TriangleMesh]) {
        let ids = Set(meshes.map(\.id))
        var changed = false
        for id in Array(tasks.keys) where !ids.contains(id) {
            tasks[id]?.cancel()
            tasks[id] = nil
        }
        for id in Array(statuses.keys) where !ids.contains(id) {
            statuses[id] = nil
            refined[id] = nil
            changed = true
        }
        for mesh in meshes where statuses[mesh.id] == nil {
            guard let source = mesh.source else { continue }
            statuses[mesh.id] = Status(resolution: mesh.resolution, isRefining: true)
            changed = true
            tasks[mesh.id] = Task { [weak self, engine] in
                await Self.refine(mesh, source: source, engine: engine) { pass, isLast in
                    self?.arrived(pass, for: mesh.id, isLast: isLast)
                }
            }
        }
        if changed { revision += 1 }
    }

    /// Stops everything and forgets it (a new document).
    func reset() {
        for task in tasks.values { task.cancel() }
        tasks = [:]
        refined = [:]
        statuses = [:]
        revision += 1
    }

    /// Returns when no mesh is being refined.
    func waitForRefinement() async {
        for task in Array(tasks.values) { await task.value }
    }

    private func arrived(_ pass: TriangleMesh?, for id: UUID, isLast: Bool) {
        guard statuses[id] != nil else { return }
        if let pass {
            refined[id] = pass
            statuses[id] = Status(resolution: pass.resolution, isRefining: !isLast)
        } else {
            statuses[id]?.isRefining = false
        }
        if isLast { tasks[id] = nil }
        revision += 1
    }

    /// Meshes `source` again, finer pass by finer pass, calling `deliver(pass, isLast)` for each; a final `deliver(nil, true)` when a
    /// pass cannot be made. Returns early, delivering nothing more, when the task is cancelled.
    private nonisolated static func refine(_ base: TriangleMesh, source: MeshSource, engine: ImplicitEngineChoice,
                                           deliver: @MainActor @escaping (TriangleMesh?, Bool) -> Void) async {
        let schedule = PreviewSchedule(source.target)
        let selection = await ImplicitEvaluators.make(for: source.solid, choice: engine)
        let mesher = ImplicitMesher(evaluator: selection.evaluator)
        var resolution = base.resolution
        var seconds = base.meshingSeconds
        guard var next = schedule.next(after: resolution, seconds: seconds) else {
            await deliver(nil, true)
            return
        }
        while !Task.isCancelled {
            do {
                let pass = try await mesher.mesh(source.solid, resolution: next, quality: .preview)
                guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
                (resolution, seconds) = (next, pass.meshingSeconds)
                let following = schedule.next(after: resolution, seconds: seconds)
                await deliver(pass, following == nil)
                guard let following else { return }
                next = following
            } catch is CancellationError {
                return
            } catch {
                // A pass that cannot be made (the shape vanished at this resolution) ends the refinement where it is.
                await deliver(nil, true)
                return
            }
        }
    }
}
````
